# Nehemiah

נחמיה

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah)

שיקום חומות ירושלים ונכונות העם לקבל עליו את שמירת המצוות.

Rebuilding Jerusalem’s walls and the nation’s commitment to observe the commandments.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Writings

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Miqra according to the Masorah (Segments: 405, License: CC-BY-SA)

Versions (En):
  - The Koren Jerusalem Bible (Segments: 405, License: CC-BY-NC)
  - The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein (Segments: 405, License: CC-BY)


## Chapter 1

###### Nehemiah 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:1)


דִּבְרֵ֥י נְחֶמְיָ֖ה בֶּן־חֲכַלְיָ֑ה וַיְהִ֤י בְחֹֽדֶשׁ־כִּסְלֵו֙ שְׁנַ֣ת עֶשְׂרִ֔ים וַאֲנִ֥י הָיִ֖יתִי בְּשׁוּשַׁ֥ן הַבִּירָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The words of Neĥemya the son of Ḥakhalya. And it came to pass in the month Kislev, in the twentieth year, as I was in Shushan the capital,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The words [written] by Nechemiah the son of Chachalyah: And it was in the month of Kislev, the twentieth year [of the reign of Artachshast (Darius)], and I was in Shushan, the capital city.


###### Nehemiah 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:2)


וַיָּבֹ֨א חֲנָ֜נִי אֶחָ֧ד מֵאַחַ֛י ה֥וּא וַאֲנָשִׁ֖ים מִֽיהוּדָ֑ה וָאֶשְׁאָלֵ֞ם עַל־הַיְּהוּדִ֧ים הַפְּלֵיטָ֛ה אֲשֶֽׁר־נִשְׁאֲר֥וּ מִן־הַשֶּׁ֖בִי וְעַל־יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that Ḥanani, one of my brothers, came, he and certain men of Yehuda; and I asked them concerning the Jews who had escaped, who were left of the captivity, and concerning Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Chanani, one of my friends, he and [other] men came from Judah, and I inquired of them about the remnant of the Jews, who were left [in Jerusalem] from the [Babylonian] captivity, and about Jerusalem.


###### Nehemiah 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:3)


וַיֹּאמְרוּ֮ לִי֒ הַֽנִּשְׁאָרִ֞ים אֲשֶֽׁר־נִשְׁאֲר֤וּ מִן־הַשְּׁבִי֙ שָׁ֣ם בַּמְּדִינָ֔ה בְּרָעָ֥ה גְדֹלָ֖ה וּבְחֶרְפָּ֑ה וְחוֹמַ֤ת יְרוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ מְפֹרָ֔צֶת וּשְׁעָרֶ֖יהָ נִצְּת֥וּ בָאֵֽשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they said to me, The remnant who are left of the captivity there in the province suffer much hardship and insult: and the wall of Yerushalayim is broken down, and its gates are burned with fire.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they answered me [(in the order of my inquiries)]: The remnant who are left of the captivity there in the land are in dire straits and in shame. And the wall of Jerusalem is breached, and its gates burnt in fire [(the Jews having restored only the Temple, but not the city walls)].


###### Nehemiah 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:4)


וַיְהִ֞י כְּשׇׁמְעִ֣י**׀** אֶת־הַדְּבָרִ֣ים הָאֵ֗לֶּה יָשַׁ֙בְתִּי֙ וָֽאֶבְכֶּ֔ה וָאֶתְאַבְּלָ֖ה יָמִ֑ים וָֽאֱהִ֥י צָם֙ וּמִתְפַּלֵּ֔ל לִפְנֵ֖י אֱלֹהֵ֥י הַשָּׁמָֽיִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass, when I heard these words, that I sat down and wept, and mourned certain days, and I fasted, and prayed before the God of heaven,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, when I heard these words, I sat and wept, and I mourned for [several] days, and I fasted and prayed before the G-d of Heaven.


###### Nehemiah 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:5)


וָאֹמַ֗ר אָֽנָּ֤א יְהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם הָאֵ֥ל הַגָּד֖וֹל וְהַנּוֹרָ֑א שֹׁמֵ֤ר הַבְּרִית֙ וָחֶ֔סֶד לְאֹהֲבָ֖יו וּלְשֹׁמְרֵ֥י מִצְוֺתָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and said, I pray thee, O Lord God of heaven, the great and terrible God, who keeps covenant and troth to those who love him and observe his commandments:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I said: I beseech You, O L-rd, G d of heaven, the great and awesome G d [viz. Daniel 9:4], keeper of the covenant and lovingkindness to His lovers and to the keepers of His mitzvoth.


###### Nehemiah 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:6)


תְּהִ֣י נָ֣א אׇזְנְךָֽ־קַשֶּׁ֣בֶת וְֽעֵינֶ֪יךָ פְתוּח֟וֹת לִשְׁמֹ֣עַ אֶל־תְּפִלַּ֣ת עַבְדְּךָ֡ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אָנֹכִי֩ מִתְפַּלֵּ֨ל לְפָנֶ֤יךָ הַיּוֹם֙ יוֹמָ֣ם וָלַ֔יְלָה עַל־בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל עֲבָדֶ֑יךָ וּמִתְוַדֶּ֗ה עַל־חַטֹּ֤אות בְּנֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר חָטָ֣אנוּ לָ֔ךְ וַאֲנִ֥י וּבֵית־אָבִ֖י חָטָֽאנוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
let Thy ear now be attentive, and Thy eyes open, that Thou mayst hear the prayer of Thy servant, which I pray before Thee now day and night, for the children of Yisra᾽el Thy servants, and I will confess the sins of the children of Yisra᾽el which we have sinned against Thee: both I and my father’s house have sinned.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
May Your ear be inclined and Your eyes open to hear the prayer of Your servant, which I pray before You this day, day and night, for the children of Israel, Your servants; and (I) make confession for the sins which the children of Israel have sinned against You, and (which) I and my father's house have sinned.


###### Nehemiah 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:7)


חֲבֹ֖ל חָבַ֣לְנוּ לָ֑ךְ וְלֹא־שָׁמַ֣רְנוּ אֶת־הַמִּצְוֺ֗ת וְאֶת־הַֽחֻקִּים֙ וְאֶת־הַמִּשְׁפָּטִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוִּ֖יתָ אֶת־מֹשֶׁ֥ה עַבְדֶּֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
We have dealt very corruptly against Thee, and have not kept the commandments, or the statutes, or the judgments, which Thou didst command Thy servant Moshe.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
We have acted corruptly against You, and we have not kept the mitzvoth and the statutes and the judgments which You commanded to Moses Your servant.


###### Nehemiah 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:8)


זְכׇר־נָא֙ אֶת־הַדָּבָ֔ר אֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוִּ֛יתָ אֶת־מֹשֶׁ֥ה עַבְדְּךָ֖ לֵאמֹ֑ר אַתֶּ֣ם תִּמְעָ֔לוּ אֲנִ֕י אָפִ֥יץ אֶתְכֶ֖ם בָּעַמִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Remember, I pray Thee, the word that Thou didst command Thy servant Moshe, saying, If you transgress, I will scatter you abroad among the nations:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Remember, now, Your charge to Moses Your servant: (If) you are faithless, I will spread you among the peoples,


###### Nehemiah 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:9)


וְשַׁבְתֶּ֣ם אֵלַ֔י וּשְׁמַרְתֶּם֙ מִצְוֺתַ֔י וַעֲשִׂיתֶ֖ם אֹתָ֑ם אִם־יִהְיֶ֨ה נִֽדַּחֲכֶ֜ם בִּקְצֵ֤ה הַשָּׁמַ֙יִם֙ מִשָּׁ֣ם אֲקַבְּצֵ֔ם (והבואתים) [וַהֲבִֽיאוֹתִים֙] אֶל־הַמָּק֔וֹם אֲשֶׁ֣ר בָּחַ֔רְתִּי לְשַׁכֵּ֥ן אֶת־שְׁמִ֖י שָֽׁם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but if you turn to me, and keep my commandments, and do them; though your outcasts be at the utmost parts of heaven, from there will I gather them, and I will bring them to the place which I have chosen to set my name there.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
but if you return to Me, and keep My mitzvoth and do them, (even) if your cast-off ones be in the end of the heavens, from there I will gather them and bring them to the place wherein I have chosen to repose My name. [(viz. Deuteronomy 30:4)].


###### Nehemiah 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:10)


וְהֵ֥ם עֲבָדֶ֖יךָ וְעַמֶּ֑ךָ אֲשֶׁ֤ר פָּדִ֙יתָ֙ בְּכֹחֲךָ֣ הַגָּד֔וֹל וּבְיָדְךָ֖ הַחֲזָקָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now these are Thy servants and Thy people, whom Thou hast redeemed by Thy great power, and by Thy strong hand.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they are Your servants and Your people that You have redeemed with Your great strength and Your strong hand.


###### Nehemiah 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/1:11)


אָנָּ֣א אֲדֹנָ֗י תְּהִ֣י נָ֣א אׇזְנְךָֽ־קַ֠שֶּׁ֠בֶת אֶל־תְּפִלַּ֨ת עַבְדְּךָ֜ וְאֶל־תְּפִלַּ֣ת עֲבָדֶ֗יךָ הַֽחֲפֵצִים֙ לְיִרְאָ֣ה אֶת־שְׁמֶ֔ךָ וְהַצְלִֽיחָה־נָּ֤א לְעַבְדְּךָ֙ הַיּ֔וֹם וּתְנֵ֣הוּ לְרַחֲמִ֔ים לִפְנֵ֖י הָאִ֣ישׁ הַזֶּ֑ה וַאֲנִ֛י הָיִ֥יתִי מַשְׁקֶ֖ה לַמֶּֽלֶךְ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O Lord, I pray Thee, let now Thy ear be attentive to the prayer of Thy servant, and to the prayer of Thy servants, who delight to fear Thy name: and prosper, I pray Thee, Thy servant this day, and grant him mercy in the sight of this man. For I was the king’s cupbearer.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I beseech you, O L-rd, let Your ear be inclined to the prayer of Your servant [(himself)] and to the prayer of Your servants [(Israel)], who desire to fear Your name. And prosper, I pray You, Your servant this day, and render him [(an object of)] compassion before this man [(Darius)]. (And I was the king's cupbearer.)



## Chapter 2

###### Nehemiah 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:1)


וַיְהִ֣י**׀** בְּחֹ֣דֶשׁ נִיסָ֗ן שְׁנַ֥ת עֶשְׂרִ֛ים לְאַרְתַּחְשַׁ֥סְתְּא הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ יַ֣יִן לְפָנָ֑יו וָאֶשָּׂ֤א אֶת־הַיַּ֙יִן֙ וָאֶתְּנָ֣ה לַמֶּ֔לֶךְ וְלֹא־הָיִ֥יתִי רַ֖ע לְפָנָֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass in the month Nisan, in the twentieth year of Artaĥshasta the king, that wine was before him: and I took up the wine, and gave it to the king. Now I had not been beforetime sad in his presence.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, in the month of Nissan, the twentieth year of Artachshast [(Darius)] the king, [they brought] wine before him [(in the courtyard)] and I took [(the cup of wine)] and gave it to the king, and I had [never before] been downcast before him [(but always cheerful)].


###### Nehemiah 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:2)


וַיֹּ֩אמֶר֩ לִ֨י הַמֶּ֜לֶךְ מַדּ֣וּעַ**׀** פָּנֶ֣יךָ רָעִ֗ים וְאַתָּה֙ אֵֽינְךָ֣ חוֹלֶ֔ה אֵ֣ין זֶ֔ה כִּי־אִ֖ם רֹ֣עַֽ לֵ֑ב וָאִירָ֖א הַרְבֵּ֥ה מְאֹֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Wherefore the king said to me, Why is thy face sad, seeing thou art not sick? this is nothing else but sorrow of heart. Then I was very much afraid,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king said to me: Why are you downcast? This must be badness of heart [i.e., you must be plotting to poison me with the wine]; and I feared greatly [(for my life)].


###### Nehemiah 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:3)


וָאֹמַ֣ר לַמֶּ֔לֶךְ הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ לְעוֹלָ֣ם יִחְיֶ֑ה מַדּ֜וּעַ לֹא־יֵרְע֣וּ פָנַ֗י אֲשֶׁ֨ר הָעִ֜יר בֵּית־קִבְר֤וֹת אֲבֹתַי֙ חֲרֵבָ֔ה וּשְׁעָרֶ֖יהָ אֻכְּל֥וּ בָאֵֽשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and I said to the king, Let the king live for ever: why should not my face be sad, when the city, the place of the tombs of my ancestors lies waste, and its gates are consumed with fire?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I said to the king: May the king live forever. Why should I not be downcast when the city of the graves of my fathers [(Jerusalem)] is in ruins, and her gates consumed with fire?


###### Nehemiah 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:4)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר לִי֙ הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ עַל־מַה־זֶּ֖ה אַתָּ֣ה מְבַקֵּ֑שׁ וָֽאֶתְפַּלֵּ֔ל אֶל־אֱלֹהֵ֖י הַשָּׁמָֽיִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the king said to me, For what dost thou make request? So I prayed to the God of heaven.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king said: What is your request [(and I shall grant it)], and I [(Nechemiah)] prayed before the G-d of heaven [(saying: So be it before the G-d of heaven that you [the king] grant my request)].


###### Nehemiah 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:5)


וָאֹמַ֣ר לַמֶּ֔לֶךְ אִם־עַל־הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ ט֔וֹב וְאִם־יִיטַ֥ב עַבְדְּךָ֖ לְפָנֶ֑יךָ אֲשֶׁ֧ר תִּשְׁלָחֵ֣נִי אֶל־יְהוּדָ֗ה אֶל־עִ֛יר קִבְר֥וֹת אֲבֹתַ֖י וְאֶבְנֶֽנָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I said to the king, If it please the king, and if thy servant have found favour in thy sight that thou wouldst send me to Yehuda, to the city of the graves of my ancestors, that I may rebuild it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I said to the king: If it pleases the king, and if your servant is worthy in your eyes, [my request is] that you send me to Judah, to the city of the graves of my fathers and that I rebuild it.


###### Nehemiah 2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:6)


וַיֹּ֩אמֶר֩ לִ֨י הַמֶּ֜לֶךְ וְהַשֵּׁגַ֣ל**׀** יוֹשֶׁ֣בֶת אֶצְל֗וֹ עַד־מָתַ֛י יִהְיֶ֥ה מַֽהֲלָכְךָ֖ וּמָתַ֣י תָּשׁ֑וּב וַיִּיטַ֤ב לִפְנֵֽי־הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ וַיִּשְׁלָחֵ֔נִי וָֽאֶתְּנָ֥ה ל֖וֹ זְמָֽן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the king said to me, (the queen also sitting by him,) For how long shall thy journey be? and when wilt thou return? So it pleased the king to send me; and I set him a time.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the king said to me (the queen sitting at his side): When do you wish to go and when will you return? And it [(my request)] was good before the king, and he sent me [i.e., he permitted me to go], and I gave him a time [(for my return)].


###### Nehemiah 2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:7)


וָאוֹמַר֮ לַמֶּ֒לֶךְ֒ אִם־עַל־הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ ט֔וֹב אִגְּרוֹת֙ יִתְּנוּ־לִ֔י עַֽל־פַּחֲו֖וֹת עֵ֣בֶר הַנָּהָ֑ר אֲשֶׁר֙ יַעֲבִיר֔וּנִי עַ֥ד אֲשֶׁר־אָב֖וֹא אֶל־יְהוּדָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover I said to the king, If it please the king, let letters be given me to the governors beyond the river, that they may let me pass through until I come into Yehuda;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I said to the king: If it pleases the king, let them [(your scribes)] give me letters [of authorization from you] to the governors across the river that they escort me [(in safety)] until I come to [Jerusalem in] Judah.


###### Nehemiah 2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:8)


וְאִגֶּ֡רֶת אֶל־אָסָף֩ שֹׁמֵ֨ר הַפַּרְדֵּ֜ס אֲשֶׁ֣ר לַמֶּ֗לֶךְ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יִתֶּן־לִ֣י עֵצִ֡ים לְ֠קָר֠וֹת אֶת־שַׁעֲרֵ֨י הַבִּירָ֤ה אֲשֶׁר־לַבַּ֙יִת֙ וּלְחוֹמַ֣ת הָעִ֔יר וְלַבַּ֖יִת אֲשֶׁר־אָב֣וֹא אֵלָ֑יו וַיִּתֶּן־לִ֣י הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ כְּיַד־אֱלֹהַ֖י הַטּוֹבָ֥ה עָלָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and a letter to Asaf the keeper of the king’s forest, that he may give me timber to make beams for the gates of the fortress of the temple, and for the wall of the city, and for the house that I shall enter into. And the king granted me, according to the good hand of my God upon me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [I request that] you send a letter to Asaf, keeper of the king's forests, that he give me timber for roofing the gates of the Temple walls and for the city walls and for the house in which I will live.


###### Nehemiah 2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:9)


וָֽאָב֗וֹא אֶֽל־פַּֽחֲווֹת֙ עֵ֣בֶר הַנָּהָ֔ר וָאֶתְּנָ֣ה לָהֶ֔ם אֵ֖ת אִגְּר֣וֹת הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וַיִּשְׁלַ֤ח עִמִּי֙ הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ שָׂ֥רֵי חַ֖יִל וּפָרָשִֽׁים׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then I came to the governors beyond the river, and gave them the king’s letters. Now the king had sent captains of the army and horsemen with me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I came to the governors across the river, and I gave them the king's letters, (and the king had sent with me officers and horsemen [to escort me]).


###### Nehemiah 2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:10)


וַיִּשְׁמַ֞ע סַנְבַלַּ֣ט הַחֹרֹנִ֗י וְטֽוֹבִיָּה֙ הָעֶ֣בֶד הָֽעַמֹּנִ֔י וַיֵּ֥רַע לָהֶ֖ם רָעָ֣ה גְדֹלָ֑ה אֲשֶׁר־בָּ֣א אָדָ֔ם לְבַקֵּ֥שׁ טוֹבָ֖ה לִבְנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When Sanvallat the Ḥoronite, and Toviyya the servant, the ῾Ammonite, heard of it, it displeased them greatly that there was come a man to seek the welfare of the children of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [when] Sanvalat the Choronite and Toviah, the Ammonite slave, heard [why I had come], it grieved them greatly that a man had come to seek the good of the children of Israel.


###### Nehemiah 2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:11)


וָאָב֖וֹא אֶל־יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וָאֱהִי־שָׁ֖ם יָמִ֥ים שְׁלֹשָֽׁה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So I came to Yerushalayim, and was there three days.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I came to Jerusalem and I stayed there for three days.


###### Nehemiah 2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:12)


וָאָק֣וּם**׀** לַ֗יְלָה אֲנִי֮ וַאֲנָשִׁ֣ים׀מְעַט֮ עִמִּי֒ וְלֹא־הִגַּ֣דְתִּי לְאָדָ֔ם מָ֗ה אֱלֹהַי֙ נֹתֵ֣ן אֶל־לִבִּ֔י לַעֲשׂ֖וֹת לִירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וּבְהֵמָה֙ אֵ֣ין עִמִּ֔י כִּ֚י אִם־הַבְּהֵמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר אֲנִ֖י רֹכֵ֥ב בָּֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I arose in the night, I and some few men with me; and I told no one what my God had put in my heart to do at Yerushalayim: nor was there any beast with me, save the beast that I rode upon.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I arose at night, I and a few men with me, and I told no one what my G-d had placed it in my heart to do with Jerusalem; and there were no beasts with me, but only the beast on which I rode [(so that they could leave without being detected and he could execute what he had in mind)].


###### Nehemiah 2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:13)


וָאֵצְאָ֨ה בְשַֽׁעַר־הַגַּ֜יְא לַ֗יְלָה וְאֶל־פְּנֵי֙ עֵ֣ין הַתַּנִּ֔ין וְאֶל־שַׁ֖עַר הָאַשְׁפֹּ֑ת וָאֱהִ֨י שֹׂבֵ֜ר בְּחוֹמֹ֤ת יְרוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ אֲשֶׁר־[הֵ֣ם׀פְּרוּצִ֔ים] (המפרוצים) וּשְׁעָרֶ֖יהָ אֻכְּל֥וּ בָאֵֽשׁ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I went out by night by the gate of the valley, even before the dragon’s well, and to the dung gate, and viewed the walls of Yerushalayim, which were broken down, and its gates which had been destroyed by fire.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I went out at night [(with the few men who were with me)] through the Gate of the Valley towards Ein Tanin and to the Dung Gate [in the walls of the city], and I breached [(even more)] the walls of Jerusalem, which were [(already)] breached and whose gates had been consumed by fire.


###### Nehemiah 2:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:14)


וָאֶֽעֱבֹר֙ אֶל־שַׁ֣עַר הָעַ֔יִן וְאֶל־בְּרֵכַ֖ת הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וְאֵין־מָק֥וֹם לַבְּהֵמָ֖ה לַעֲבֹ֥ר תַּחְתָּֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then I went on to the fountain gate and to the king’s pool: but there was no place for the beast that was under me to pass.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I [wished to] pass on to the Spring Gate and to the King's pool, but there was no room [(in those breaches)] for the beast under me to pass on.


###### Nehemiah 2:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:15)


וָאֱהִ֨י עֹלֶ֤ה בַנַּ֙חַל֙ לַ֔יְלָה וָאֱהִ֥י שֹׂבֵ֖ר בַּחוֹמָ֑ה וָאָשׁ֗וּב וָאָב֛וֹא בְּשַׁ֥עַר הַגַּ֖יְא וָאָשֽׁוּב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then I went up in the night by the wadi, and viewed the wall, and turned back, and entered by the gate of the valley, and so returned.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
So I went up the valley at night, breaking the wall [(with the men who were with me)], and I returned and came to the Gate of the Valley, and I returned [(whence I had come)].


###### Nehemiah 2:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:16)


וְהַסְּגָנִ֗ים לֹ֤א יָדְעוּ֙ אָ֣נָה הָלַ֔כְתִּי וּמָ֖ה אֲנִ֣י עֹשֶׂ֑ה וְלַיְּהוּדִ֨ים וְלַכֹּהֲנִ֜ים וְלַחֹרִ֣ים וְלַסְּגָנִ֗ים וּלְיֶ֙תֶר֙ עֹשֵׂ֣ה הַמְּלָאכָ֔ה עַד־כֵּ֖ן לֹ֥א הִגַּֽדְתִּי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the rulers knew not where I had gone or what I had done; nor had I as yet told it to the Jews, or to the priests, or to the nobles, or to the rulers, or to the rest who did the work.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the [city] officers did not know where I had gone and what I had done, and I had not told (of it) until then to the Jews, the priests, the nobles, the officers, and the rest of the functionaries.


###### Nehemiah 2:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:17)


וָאוֹמַ֣ר אֲלֵהֶ֗ם אַתֶּ֤ם רֹאִים֙ הָרָעָה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אֲנַ֣חְנוּ בָ֔הּ אֲשֶׁ֤ר יְרוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ חֲרֵבָ֔ה וּשְׁעָרֶ֖יהָ נִצְּת֣וּ בָאֵ֑שׁ לְכ֗וּ וְנִבְנֶה֙ אֶת־חוֹמַ֣ת יְרוּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וְלֹא־נִהְיֶ֥ה ע֖וֹד חֶרְפָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then said I to them, You see the distress that we are in, how Yerushalayim lies waste, and its gates are burned with fire: come and let us build up the wall of Yerushalayim, so that we no longer suffer insult.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [(the next day)] I said to them: You see the evil in which we find ourselves, that Jerusalem is in ruins and its gates consumed in fire. Let us go and build the wall of Jerusalem and not be [reduced to] shame anymore.


###### Nehemiah 2:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:18)


וָאַגִּ֨יד לָהֶ֜ם אֶת־יַ֣ד אֱלֹהַ֗י אֲשֶׁר־הִיא֙ טוֹבָ֣ה עָלַ֔י וְאַף־דִּבְרֵ֥י הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אָֽמַר־לִ֑י וַיֹּֽאמְרוּ֙ נָק֣וּם וּבָנִ֔ינוּ וַיְחַזְּק֥וּ יְדֵיהֶ֖ם לַטּוֹבָֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then I told them of the hand of my God which was good upon me; as also the king’s words that had spoken to me. And they said, Let us rise up and build. So they strengthened their hands for this good work.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I told them of the hand of my G-d upon me for the good [(i.e., His having granted me favor in the eyes of the king)], and also the words of the king that he had spoken to me. And they said: Let us arise and build! And they strengthened their hands for the good [i.e., for the building of the wall and the towers of the city.]


###### Nehemiah 2:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:19)


וַיִּשְׁמַע֩ סַנְבַלַּ֨ט הַחֹרֹנִ֜י וְטֹבִיָּ֣ה**׀** הָעֶ֣בֶד הָֽעַמּוֹנִ֗י וְגֶ֙שֶׁם֙ הָֽעַרְבִ֔י וַיַּלְעִ֣גוּ לָ֔נוּ וַיִּבְז֖וּ עָלֵ֑ינוּ וַיֹּאמְר֗וּ מָֽה־הַדָּבָ֤ר הַזֶּה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אַתֶּ֣ם עֹשִׂ֔ים הַעַ֥ל הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ אַתֶּ֥ם מֹרְדִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But when Sanvallat the Ḥoronite, and Toviyya the servant, the ῾Ammonite, and Geshem the ῾Arvian heard it, they laughed us to scorn, and despised us, and said, What is this thing that you do? will you rebel against the king?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Sanvalat the Choronite and Toviah the Ammonite slave and Geshem the Arab heard, and they mocked and scorned us, saying: What is this thing that you do? Would you rebel against the king!


###### Nehemiah 2:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/2:20)


וָאָשִׁ֨יב אוֹתָ֜ם דָּבָ֗ר וָאוֹמַ֤ר לָהֶם֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י הַשָּׁמַ֗יִם ה֚וּא יַצְלִ֣יחַֽ לָ֔נוּ וַאֲנַ֥חְנוּ עֲבָדָ֖יו נָק֣וּם וּבָנִ֑ינוּ וְלָכֶ֗ם אֵֽין־חֵ֧לֶק וּצְדָקָ֛ה וְזִכָּר֖וֹן בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then I answered them, and said to them, The God of heaven, he will prosper us; therefore we his servants will arise and build: but you have no portion, or right, or memorial, in Yerushalayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I rejoined: The G-d of heaven, He will prosper us, and we, His servants, will arise and build. As for you, [what business do you have with building the city wall]? You have no share or righteousness or remembrance in Jerusalem!



## Chapter 3

###### Nehemiah 3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:1)


וַיָּ֡קׇם אֶלְיָשִׁיב֩ הַכֹּהֵ֨ן הַגָּד֜וֹל וְאֶחָ֣יו הַכֹּהֲנִ֗ים וַיִּבְנוּ֙ אֶת־שַׁ֣עַר הַצֹּ֔אן הֵ֣מָּה קִדְּשׁ֔וּהוּ וַֽיַּעֲמִ֖ידוּ דַּלְתֹתָ֑יו וְעַד־מִגְדַּ֤ל הַמֵּאָה֙ קִדְּשׁ֔וּהוּ עַ֖ד מִגְדַּ֥ל חֲנַנְאֵֽל׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Elyashiv the high priest rose up with his brethren the priests, and they built the sheep gate; they sanctified it, and set up its doors; even to the tower of the hundred they sanctified it, as far as the tower of Ḥanan᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Elyashiv the high-priest arose with his brother priests and they built the Sheep Gate. They sanctified it [(with the sanctity of the city)], and they set up its doors [(the doors of the gate)]. And they sanctified it until the Tower of the Hundred, until the Tower of Chananel.


###### Nehemiah 3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:2)


וְעַל־יָד֥וֹ בָנ֖וּ אַנְשֵׁ֣י יְרֵח֑וֹ וְעַל־יָד֣וֹ בָנָ֔ה זַכּ֖וּר בֶּן־אִמְרִֽי׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And next to him the men of Yereĥo built. And next to them Zakkur the son of Imri built.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And near him [i.e., near every builder who built the Sheep Gate], the men of Yereicho built [the portion assigned to them]. And near him [i.e., near each of the men of Yereicho], Zakkur the son of Imri built.


###### Nehemiah 3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:3)


וְאֵת֙ שַׁ֣עַר הַדָּגִ֔ים בָּנ֖וּ בְּנֵ֣י הַסְּנָאָ֑ה הֵ֣מָּה קֵר֔וּהוּ וַֽיַּעֲמִ֙ידוּ֙ דַּלְתֹתָ֔יו מַנְעוּלָ֖יו וּבְרִיחָֽיו׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the fish gate did the sons of Sena᾽a build; they also laid its beams, and set up its doors, its bolts, and its bars.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Fish Gate was built by the sons of Hassna'ah. They built it and set up its doors, its locks and its bars.


###### Nehemiah 3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:4)


וְעַל־יָדָ֣ם הֶחֱזִ֗יק מְרֵמ֤וֹת בֶּן־אוּרִיָּה֙ בֶּן־הַקּ֔וֹץ{ס}וְעַל־יָדָ֣ם הֶחֱזִ֔יק מְשֻׁלָּ֥ם בֶּן־בֶּרֶכְיָ֖ה בֶּן־מְשֵׁיזַבְאֵ֑ל{ס}וְעַל־יָדָ֣ם הֶֽחֱזִ֔יק צָד֖וֹק בֶּֽן־בַּעֲנָֽא׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And next to them Meremot the son of Uriyya, the son of Qoż repaired. And next to them Meshullam the son of Berekhya the son of Meshezav᾽el repaired. And next to them Żadoq the son of Ba῾ana repaired.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And near them adding [(to the wall)], were Meremoth, the son of Uriah, the son of Hakotz [and his men]; and near them, adding, were Meshulam, the son of Berechyah, the son of Meshezavel; and near them, adding, was Tzaddok the son of Ba'anah.


###### Nehemiah 3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:5)


וְעַל־יָדָ֖ם הֶחֱזִ֣יקוּ הַתְּקוֹעִ֑ים וְאַדִּֽירֵיהֶם֙ לֹא־הֵבִ֣יאוּ צַוָּרָ֔ם בַּעֲבֹדַ֖ת אֲדֹנֵיהֶֽם׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And next to them the Teqo῾ites repaired; but their nobles did not put their necks to the work of their Lord.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And near them, adding, were the Tekoites. But their wealthy ones did not place their necks under the labor of their L-rd.


###### Nehemiah 3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:6)


וְאֵת֩ שַׁ֨עַר הַיְשָׁנָ֜ה הֶחֱזִ֗יקוּ יֽוֹיָדָע֙ בֶּן־פָּסֵ֔חַ וּמְשֻׁלָּ֖ם בֶּן־בְּסֽוֹדְיָ֑ה הֵ֣מָּה קֵר֔וּהוּ וַֽיַּעֲמִ֙ידוּ֙ דַּלְתֹתָ֔יו וּמַנְעֻלָ֖יו וּבְרִיחָֽיו׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Yoyada the son of Paseaĥ, and Meshullam the son of Besodya repaired the old gate; they laid its beams, and set up its doors, and its bolts, and its bars.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Old Gate was restored by Yoyada the son of Paseach and Meshulam the son of Besodyah. They roofed it and set up its doors and its locks and its bars.


###### Nehemiah 3:7

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Above the Horse Gate, the priests added, each opposite his house.


###### Nehemiah 3:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:29)


אַחֲרָ֧יו הֶחֱזִ֛יק צָד֥וֹק בֶּן־אִמֵּ֖ר נֶ֣גֶד בֵּית֑וֹ{ס}וְאַחֲרָ֤יו הֶחֱזִיק֙ שְׁמַֽעְיָ֣ה בֶן־שְׁכַנְיָ֔ה שֹׁמֵ֖ר שַׁ֥עַר הַמִּזְרָֽח׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
After them Żadoq the son of Immer repaired opposite his house. And after him Shema῾ya the son of Shekhanya, the keeper of the east gate, repaired.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
After them, Tzaddok the son of Immer added opposite his house, and after him, Shemayah the son of Shechanyah, keeper of the eastern gate, added.


###### Nehemiah 3:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:30)


(אחרי) [אַחֲרָ֨יו] הֶחֱזִ֜יק חֲנַנְיָ֣ה בֶן־שֶׁלֶמְיָ֗ה וְחָנ֧וּן בֶּן־צָלָ֛ף הַשִּׁשִּׁ֖י מִדָּ֣ה שֵׁנִ֑י{ס}אַחֲרָ֣יו הֶחֱזִ֗יק מְשֻׁלָּם֙ בֶּן־בֶּ֣רֶכְיָ֔ה נֶ֖גֶד נִשְׁכָּתֽוֹ׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
After him Ḥananya the son of Shelemya, and Ḥanun the sixth son of Żalaf repaired another section. After him Meshullam the son of Berekhya repaired opposite his chamber.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
After him, Chananiah the son of Shelemyah and Chanun the sixth son of Tzalaf added a second measure. After them, Meshulam the son of Berechyah added opposite his quarters.


###### Nehemiah 3:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:31)


(אחרי) [אַחֲרָ֣יו] הֶחֱזִ֗יק מַלְכִּיָּה֙ בֶּן־הַצֹּ֣רְפִ֔י עַד־בֵּ֥ית הַנְּתִינִ֖ים וְהָרֹכְלִ֑ים נֶ֚גֶד שַׁ֣עַר הַמִּפְקָ֔ד וְעַ֖ד עֲלִיַּ֥ת הַפִּנָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
After him Malkiyya one of the goldsmiths repaired as far as the house of the temple servants and of the merchants, opposite the muster gate to the corner of the battlement.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
After him, Malkiyah the son of the Tzorfi added until the house of the Nethinim and the merchants, opposite the Mifkad Gate, and until the ascent of the Pinah.


###### Nehemiah 3:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:32)


וּבֵ֨ין עֲלִיַּ֤ת הַפִּנָּה֙ לְשַׁ֣עַר הַצֹּ֔אן הֶחֱזִ֥יקוּ הַצֹּרְפִ֖ים וְהָרֹכְלִֽים׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And between the corner of the battlement and the sheep gate the goldsmiths and the merchants repaired.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And between the ascent of the Pinah until the Sheep Gate [(where the wall had begun [viz. verse 1])], the smiths and the merchants added.


###### Nehemiah 3:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:33)


וַיְהִ֞י כַּאֲשֶׁ֧ר שָׁמַ֣ע סַנְבַלַּ֗ט כִּֽי־אֲנַ֤חְנוּ בוֹנִים֙ אֶת־הַ֣חוֹמָ֔ה וַיִּ֣חַר ל֔וֹ וַיִּכְעַ֖ס הַרְבֵּ֑ה וַיַּלְעֵ֖ג עַל־הַיְּהוּדִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But it came to pass, that when Sanvallat heard that we were building the wall, he was angry, and greatly enraged, and he mocked the Jews.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, when Sanvalet heard that we were building the wall, he was irate and extremely wroth, and he mocked the Jews.


###### Nehemiah 3:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:34)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר**׀** לִפְנֵ֣י אֶחָ֗יו וְחֵיל֙ שֹֽׁמְר֔וֹן וַיֹּ֕אמֶר מָ֛ה הַיְּהוּדִ֥ים הָאֲמֵלָלִ֖ים עֹשִׂ֑ים הֲיַעַזְב֨וּ לָהֶ֤ם הֲיִזְבָּ֙חוּ֙ הַיְכַלּ֣וּ בַיּ֔וֹם הַיְחַיּ֧וּ אֶת־הָאֲבָנִ֛ים מֵעֲרֵמ֥וֹת הֶעָפָ֖ר וְהֵ֥מָּה שְׂרוּפֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he spoke before his brethren and the army of Shomeron, and said, What are these feeble Jews doing? will they restore things? will they sacrifice? will they make an end in a day? will they revive the stones out of the heaps of the rubbish seeing they are burned?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said before his brothers and the host of Shomron [(oppressors of Judah)]. What are these debilitated Jews doing? Will they [(the idolators)] allow them [(to continue to build)]? Will they [eternally] sacrifice [their offerings]? Will they finish [their work] in [one] day [(so that the idolators not halt it)]! Will they resurrect the stones [they are building with] from the piles of earth [where they found them fallen] when they are [completely] burnt!


###### Nehemiah 3:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:35)


וְטוֹבִיָּ֥ה הָעַמֹּנִ֖י אֶצְל֑וֹ וַיֹּ֗אמֶר גַּ֚ם אֲשֶׁר־הֵ֣ם בּוֹנִ֔ים אִם־יַעֲלֶ֣ה שׁוּעָ֔ל וּפָרַ֖ץ חוֹמַ֥ת אַבְנֵיהֶֽם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now Toviyya the ῾Ammonite was by him, and he said, Even that which they build, if a fox go up, he shall break down their stone wall.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Toviah the Ammonite was at his side, and he said: Even if a wolf go up against what they are building he will breach the wall of their stones!


###### Nehemiah 3:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:36)


שְׁמַ֤ע אֱלֹהֵ֙ינוּ֙ כִּֽי־הָיִ֣ינוּ בוּזָ֔ה וְהָשֵׁ֥ב חֶרְפָּתָ֖ם אֶל־רֹאשָׁ֑ם וּתְנֵ֥ם לְבִזָּ֖ה בְּאֶ֥רֶץ שִׁבְיָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hear, O our God; for we are despised: and turn their insolence upon their own head, and give them up for a prey in the land of captivity:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(When I heard their mockery, I prayed)]: Hear, our G-d, for we have been made [(objects of)] shame, and return their insults to their heads, and reduce them to shame in the land of [their] captivity [(when they are exiled)]!


###### Nehemiah 3:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:37)


וְאַל־תְּכַס֙ עַל־עֲוֺנָ֔ם וְחַטָּאתָ֖ם מִלְּפָנֶ֣יךָ אַל־תִּמָּחֶ֑ה כִּ֥י הִכְעִ֖יסוּ לְנֶ֥גֶד הַבּוֹנִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and do not cover their iniquity, and do not let their sin be blotted out from before Thee: for they have provoked Thee to anger before the builders.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And do not cover up their transgressions, and do not erase their sins from before You, for they have vented their anger at the builders [(by mocking them)]!


###### Nehemiah 3:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/3:38)


וַנִּבְנֶה֙ אֶת־הַ֣חוֹמָ֔ה וַתִּקָּשֵׁ֥ר כׇּל־הַחוֹמָ֖ה עַד־חֶצְיָ֑הּ וַיְהִ֧י לֵ֦ב לָעָ֖ם לַעֲשֽׂוֹת׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So we built the wall; and all the wall was joined together to half its height: for the people had a mind to work.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And we [continued] building the wall, and when [the breaches of] half the wall had been filled, the heart of the people was [inspired] to do [i.e., to complete it].



## Chapter 4

###### Nehemiah 4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:1)


וַיְהִ֣י כַאֲשֶׁ֣ר שָׁמַ֣ע סַנְבַלַּ֡ט וְ֠טוֹבִיָּ֠ה וְהָעַרְבִ֨ים וְהָעַמֹּנִ֜ים וְהָאַשְׁדּוֹדִ֗ים כִּֽי־עָלְתָ֤ה אֲרוּכָה֙ לְחֹמ֣וֹת יְרוּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם כִּי־הֵחֵ֥לּוּ הַפְּרֻצִ֖ים לְהִסָּתֵ֑ם וַיִּ֥חַר לָהֶ֖ם מְאֹֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But it came to pass, that when Sanvallat, and Toviyya, and the ῾Arvians, and the ῾Ammonites, and the Ashdodites, heard that the walls of Yerushalayim were being repaired, and that the breaches began to be stopped, then they were very angry,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, when Sanvalet and Toviah and the Arabs and the Ammonites and the Ashdodites heard that the walls of Jerusalem had been "healed," that the "breached ones" [(the Jews of the city)] had [now] been closed in, they were extremely wroth.


###### Nehemiah 4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:2)


וַיִּקְשְׁר֤וּ כֻלָּם֙ יַחְדָּ֔ו לָב֖וֹא לְהִלָּחֵ֣ם בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְלַעֲשׂ֥וֹת ל֖וֹ תּוֹעָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and they conspired all of them together to come and to fight against Yerushalayim, and to cause confusion in it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they all banded together to come to war against Jerusalem and to render it [(the wall)] a ruin.


###### Nehemiah 4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:3)


וַנִּתְפַּלֵּ֖ל אֶל־אֱלֹהֵ֑ינוּ וַנַּעֲמִ֨יד מִשְׁמָ֧ר עֲלֵיהֶ֛ם יוֹמָ֥ם וָלַ֖יְלָה מִפְּנֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Nevertheless we made our prayer to our God, and set a watch against them day and night, because of them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And we prayed to our G-d, and we set up a guard for them [(the builders)] day and night against them [(the invaders)].


###### Nehemiah 4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:4)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר יְהוּדָ֗ה כָּשַׁל֙ כֹּ֣חַ הַסַּבָּ֔ל וְהֶעָפָ֖ר הַרְבֵּ֑ה וַאֲנַ֙חְנוּ֙ לֹ֣א נוּכַ֔ל לִבְנ֖וֹת בַּחוֹמָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Yehuda said, The strength of the bearers of burdens is failing, and there is much rubbish; so that we are not able to build the wall.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Judah said [(in fear of the foe)]: The strength of the workers has waned and the earth [(for building)] is much [i.e., there is yet much for us to build], and we shall not be able to build the wall.


###### Nehemiah 4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:5)


וַיֹּאמְר֣וּ צָרֵ֗ינוּ לֹ֤א יֵדְעוּ֙ וְלֹ֣א יִרְא֔וּ עַ֛ד אֲשֶׁר־נָב֥וֹא אֶל־תּוֹכָ֖ם וַהֲרַגְנ֑וּם וְהִשְׁבַּ֖תְנוּ אֶת־הַמְּלָאכָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And our adversaries said, They shall neither know, nor see, until we come in the midst among them, and slay them, and cause the work to cease.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(Nechemiah says what follows:)] And our foes said: They [(Israel)] will not know [(of our presence)] and they will not see until we come into their midst and we will halt the work.


###### Nehemiah 4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:6)


וַֽיְהִי֙ כַּאֲשֶׁר־בָּ֣אוּ הַיְּהוּדִ֔ים הַיֹּשְׁבִ֖ים אֶצְלָ֑ם וַיֹּ֤אמְרוּ לָ֙נוּ֙ עֶ֣שֶׂר פְּעָמִ֔ים מִכׇּל־הַמְּקֹמ֖וֹת אֲשֶׁר־תָּשׁ֥וּבוּ עָלֵֽינוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass, that when the Jews who dwelt by them came, they said to us ten times, From all places where you shall return, they will be upon us.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, when the Jews who dwelt with them came and told us ten times: From all the places [(where you find yourselves engaged in the work of the wall, assemble when they attack until)] you return upon us [(to fight us)].


###### Nehemiah 4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:7)


וָֽאַעֲמִ֞יד מִֽתַּחְתְּי֧וֹת לַמָּק֛וֹם מֵאַחֲרֵ֥י לַחוֹמָ֖ה (בצחחיים) [בַּצְּחִיחִ֑ים] וָֽאַעֲמִ֤יד אֶת־הָעָם֙ לְמִשְׁפָּח֔וֹת עִם־חַרְבֹתֵיהֶ֛ם רׇמְחֵיהֶ֖ם וְקַשְּׁתֹתֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore in the lower places behind the wall on the bare face of the rock, I stationed the people; I stationed them according to their families, with their swords, their spears, and their bows.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I positioned beneath that place [(around Jerusalem)] behind the wall [(on the outside)] (and) on the rocks — I positioned the people by [the strongest of] their families with their swords, their spears, and their bows.


###### Nehemiah 4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:8)


וָאֵ֣רֶא וָאָק֗וּם וָאֹמַ֞ר אֶל־הַחֹרִ֤ים וְאֶל־הַסְּגָנִים֙ וְאֶל־יֶ֣תֶר הָעָ֔ם אַל־תִּֽירְא֖וּ מִפְּנֵיהֶ֑ם אֶת־אֲדֹנָ֞י הַגָּד֤וֹל וְהַנּוֹרָא֙ זְכֹ֔רוּ וְהִֽלָּחֲמ֗וּ עַל־אֲחֵיכֶם֙ בְּנֵיכֶ֣ם וּבְנֹתֵיכֶ֔ם נְשֵׁיכֶ֖ם וּבָתֵּיכֶֽם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I looked, and rose up, and said to the nobles, and to the rulers, and to the rest of the people, Be not afraid of them: remember the Lord, who is great and awe inspiring, and fight for your brethren, your sons, and your daughters, your wives, and your houses.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when I saw [(the foe approaching)], I arose and said to the nobles, the officers, and the rest of the people: Do not fear them. Remember the great, awesome L-rd, and fight for your brothers, your sons, your daughters, your wives, and your homes!


###### Nehemiah 4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:9)


וַיְהִ֞י כַּֽאֲשֶׁר־שָׁמְע֤וּ אוֹיְבֵ֙ינוּ֙ כִּי־נ֣וֹדַֽע לָ֔נוּ וַיָּ֥פֶר הָאֱלֹהִ֖ים אֶת־עֲצָתָ֑ם (ונשוב) [וַנָּ֤שׇׁב] כֻּלָּ֙נוּ֙ אֶל־הַ֣חוֹמָ֔ה אִ֖ישׁ אֶל־מְלַאכְתּֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass, when our enemies heard that it was known to us, and God had brought their counsel to nought, that we returned all of us to the wall, everyone to his work.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, when our foes heard that [their intent] was known to us, G-d destroyed their counsel and we all returned to the wall, each to his work.


###### Nehemiah 4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:10)


וַיְהִ֣י**׀** מִן־הַיּ֣וֹם הַה֗וּא חֲצִ֣י נְעָרַי֮ עֹשִׂ֣ים בַּמְּלָאכָה֒ וְחֶצְיָ֗ם מַחֲזִיקִים֙ וְהָרְמָחִ֣ים הַמָּגִנִּ֔ים וְהַקְּשָׁת֖וֹת וְהַשִּׁרְיֹנִ֑ים וְהַ֨שָּׂרִ֔ים אַחֲרֵ֖י כׇּל־בֵּ֥ית יְהוּדָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass from that time onwards, that the half of my servants did the work, and the other half of them held the spears, the shields, and the bows, and the coats of mail; and the rulers were behind all the house of Yehuda.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, from that day on, that half of my [strong] youths did the work [on the wall] while the [other] half held the spears, the shields, the bows and the armor. And the chiefs backed up all [the tribes of] the house of Judah.


###### Nehemiah 4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:11)


הַבּוֹנִ֧ים בַּחוֹמָ֛ה וְהַנֹּשְׂאִ֥ים בַּסֶּ֖בֶל עֹמְשִׂ֑ים בְּאַחַ֤ת יָדוֹ֙ עֹשֶׂ֣ה בַמְּלָאכָ֔ה וְאַחַ֖ת מַחֲזֶ֥קֶת הַשָּֽׁלַח׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They who built the wall, and they who bore burdens, loaded themselves in such a way that with one of his hands each laboured in the work, and with the other hand he held a weapon.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The builders of the wall and the carriers of the loads, with one hand did their work, and with the other held a weapon.


###### Nehemiah 4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:12)


וְהַ֨בּוֹנִ֔ים אִ֥ישׁ חַרְבּ֛וֹ אֲסוּרִ֥ים עַל־מׇתְנָ֖יו וּבוֹנִ֑ים וְהַתּוֹקֵ֥עַ בַּשּׁוֹפָ֖ר אֶצְלִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the builders, everyone had his sword girded by his side, and so he built. And he who sounded the shofar was by me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And each man built, with his sword tied to his thighs; and the blower of the shofar [(to alert the men in case of an attack)] was [always] at my side.


###### Nehemiah 4:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:13)


וָאֹמַ֞ר אֶל־הַחֹרִ֤ים וְאֶל־הַסְּגָנִים֙ וְאֶל־יֶ֣תֶר הָעָ֔ם הַמְּלָאכָ֥ה הַרְבֵּ֖ה וּרְחָבָ֑ה וַאֲנַ֗חְנוּ נִפְרָדִים֙ עַל־הַ֣חוֹמָ֔ה רְחוֹקִ֖ים אִ֥ישׁ מֵאָחִֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I said to the nobles, and to the rulers, and to the rest of the people, The work is great and large, and we are separated upon the wall, far from one another.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I said to the nobles and to the officers and to the rest of the people: The work is great and wide [spread], and we are separated on the wall, distant, one from the other.


###### Nehemiah 4:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:14)


בִּמְק֗וֹם אֲשֶׁ֤ר תִּשְׁמְעוּ֙ אֶת־ק֣וֹל הַשּׁוֹפָ֔ר שָׁ֖מָּה תִּקָּבְצ֣וּ אֵלֵ֑ינוּ אֱלֹהֵ֖ינוּ יִלָּ֥חֶם לָֽנוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
In whatever place you hear the sound of the shofar, rally to us there: our God shall fight for us.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the place where you hear the sound of the shofar, there shall you gather to us, (and) G-d shall fight for us.


###### Nehemiah 4:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:15)


וַאֲנַ֖חְנוּ עֹשִׂ֣ים בַּמְּלָאכָ֑ה וְחֶצְיָ֗ם מַחֲזִיקִים֙ בָּֽרְמָחִ֔ים מֵעֲל֣וֹת הַשַּׁ֔חַר עַ֖ד צֵ֥את הַכּוֹכָבִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So we laboured in the work: and half of them held the spears from the rising of the morning till the stars appeared.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And we did the work, and half held the spears, from dawn until dusk.


###### Nehemiah 4:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:16)


גַּ֣ם בָּעֵ֤ת הַהִיא֙ אָמַ֣רְתִּי לָעָ֔ם אִ֣ישׁ וְנַעֲר֔וֹ יָלִ֖ינוּ בְּת֣וֹךְ יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְהָֽיוּ־לָ֧נוּ הַלַּ֛יְלָה מִשְׁמָ֖ר וְהַיּ֥וֹם מְלָאכָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Likewise at the same time I said to the people, Let everyone with his servant lodge within Yerushalayim, so that in the night they may be a guard to us, and labour in the day.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Also at that time I said to the people: Let each man and his youth spend the night in Jerusalem — so that the night was for us for guarding, and the day for work.


###### Nehemiah 4:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/4:17)


וְאֵ֨ין אֲנִ֜י וְאַחַ֣י וּנְעָרַ֗י וְאַנְשֵׁ֤י הַמִּשְׁמָר֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אַחֲרַ֔י אֵין־אֲנַ֥חְנוּ פֹשְׁטִ֖ים בְּגָדֵ֑ינוּ אִ֖ישׁ שִׁלְח֥וֹ הַמָּֽיִם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So neither I, nor my brethren, nor my servants, nor the men of the guard who followed me, none of us put off our clothes: everyone brought his weapon with him, even to the water.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And neither I, nor my brethren, nor my youths, nor the men of the watch after me took off our clothes [to sleep] or [to wash them] in water.



## Chapter 5

###### Nehemiah 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:1)


וַתְּהִ֨י צַעֲקַ֥ת הָעָ֛ם וּנְשֵׁיהֶ֖ם גְּדוֹלָ֑ה אֶל־אֲחֵיהֶ֖ם הַיְּהוּדִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And there was a great cry of the people and of their wives against their brethren the Jews.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the outcry of [the poor of] the people and their wives was great against their brother Jews [(the rich, who refused to feed them)].


###### Nehemiah 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:2)


וְיֵשׁ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אֹמְרִ֔ים בָּנֵ֥ינוּ וּבְנֹתֵ֖ינוּ אֲנַ֣חְנוּ רַבִּ֑ים וְנִקְחָ֥ה דָגָ֖ן וְנֹאכְלָ֥ה וְנִחְיֶֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For there were those who said, We, our sons, and our daughters, are many: therefore let us get corn, that we may eat, and live.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Some of them said [(i.e., they cried out to the rich Jews)]: [With] our sons and daughters we are [too] many [to survive. We will have to sell them] to buy grain in order to eat and live!


###### Nehemiah 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:3)


וְיֵשׁ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אֹמְרִ֔ים שְׂדֹתֵ֛ינוּ וּכְרָמֵ֥ינוּ וּבָתֵּ֖ינוּ אֲנַ֣חְנוּ עֹרְבִ֑ים וְנִקְחָ֥ה דָגָ֖ן בָּרָעָֽב׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Some also there were who said, We have mortgaged our lands, our vineyards, and our houses, that we might buy corn, because of the dearth.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And some said: We will [have to] pawn our fields and our vineyards and our houses to buy grain in (our) hunger!


###### Nehemiah 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:4)


וְיֵשׁ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אֹמְרִ֔ים לָוִ֥ינוּ כֶ֖סֶף לְמִדַּ֣ת הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ שְׂדֹתֵ֖ינוּ וּכְרָמֵֽינוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There were also those who said, We have borrowed money for the king’s tribute, and that upon our lands and vineyards.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And some said: We have [already] borrowed money (on) our fields and vineyards to [pay] the king's taxes [(and we have nothing left to feed ourselves with)]!


###### Nehemiah 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:5)


וְעַתָּ֗ה כִּבְשַׂ֤ר אַחֵ֙ינוּ֙ בְּשָׂרֵ֔נוּ כִּבְנֵיהֶ֖ם בָּנֵ֑ינוּ וְהִנֵּ֣ה אֲנַ֣חְנוּ כֹ֠בְשִׁ֠ים אֶת־בָּנֵ֨ינוּ וְאֶת־בְּנֹתֵ֜ינוּ לַעֲבָדִ֗ים וְיֵ֨שׁ מִבְּנֹתֵ֤ינוּ נִכְבָּשׁוֹת֙ וְאֵ֣ין לְאֵ֣ל יָדֵ֔נוּ וּשְׂדֹתֵ֥ינוּ וּכְרָמֵ֖ינוּ לַאֲחֵרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yet now our flesh is as good as the flesh of our brethren, our children as good as their children: and, lo, we press our sons and our daughters into slavery, and some of our daughters are pressed into slavery already: nor is it in our power to redeem them; for other men have our lands and vineyards.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, [just as good] as our [rich] brothers' flesh is our flesh! [Just as good as] their sons are our sons! And, behold, we will have to impress our sons and daughters as slaves; and some of our daughters are [already] impressed, and we have not the wherewithal [to redeem them]. And our fields and vineyards [have already been given] to others [for food, and what shall we eat now!]


###### Nehemiah 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:6)


וַיִּ֥חַר לִ֖י מְאֹ֑ד כַּאֲשֶׁ֤ר שָׁמַ֙עְתִּי֙ אֶת־זַֽעֲקָתָ֔ם וְאֵ֖ת הַדְּבָרִ֥ים הָאֵֽלֶּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I was very angry when I heard their cry and these words.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I was greatly troubled when I heard their cries and these things.


###### Nehemiah 5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:7)


וַיִּמָּלֵ֨ךְ לִבִּ֜י עָלַ֗י וָאָרִ֙יבָה֙ אֶת־הַחֹרִ֣ים וְאֶת־הַסְּגָנִ֔ים וָאֹמְרָ֣ה לָהֶ֔ם מַשָּׁ֥א אִישׁ־בְּאָחִ֖יו אַתֶּ֣ם (נשאים) [נֹשִׁ֑ים] וָאֶתֵּ֥ן עֲלֵיהֶ֖ם קְהִלָּ֥ה גְדוֹלָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then I took counsel with myself, and I rebuked the nobles, and the rulers, and said to them, You make claims for debt, everyone of his brother! And I held a great assembly against them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And my heart counseled me, and I quarreled with the nobles and the officers and said to them: You are exacting payment, each of his brother, [(by lending the poor in order to impress their sons and daughters and take their fields, their vineyards, and their houses.)] And I congregated against them a great crowd [(of the poor to cry out against them and shame them.)]


###### Nehemiah 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:8)


וָאֹמְרָ֣ה לָהֶ֗ם אֲנַ֣חְנוּ קָ֠נִ֠ינוּ אֶת־אַחֵ֨ינוּ הַיְּהוּדִ֜ים הַנִּמְכָּרִ֤ים לַגּוֹיִם֙ כְּדֵ֣י בָ֔נוּ וְגַם־אַתֶּ֛ם תִּמְכְּר֥וּ אֶת־אֲחֵיכֶ֖ם וְנִמְכְּרוּ־לָ֑נוּ וַֽיַּחֲרִ֔ישׁוּ וְלֹ֥א מָצְא֖וּ דָּבָֽר׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I said to them, We after our ability have redeemed our brethren the Jews, who were sold to the heathen; and will you nevertheless sell your brethren? or shall they be sold to us? Then they held their peace, and found nothing to answer.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I said to them: We have [already] bought back our brother Jews [in captivity] who had been sold to the gentiles, with whatever [money] we had; yet you, too, would sell your brothers [impressed under you, to the gentiles], and [in the end] they would be sold to us [i.e., we would have to buy them back!] And they were [struck] silent [in shame] and they found nothing [to say].


###### Nehemiah 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:9)


(ויאמר) [וָאוֹמַ֕ר] לֹא־ט֥וֹב הַדָּבָ֖ר אֲשֶׁר־אַתֶּ֣ם עֹשִׂ֑ים הֲל֞וֹא בְּיִרְאַ֤ת אֱלֹהֵ֙ינוּ֙ תֵּלֵ֔כוּ מֵחֶרְפַּ֖ת הַגּוֹיִ֥ם אוֹיְבֵֽינוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Also I said, That which you do is not good: ought you not to walk in the fear of our God because of the insult of the heathen our enemies?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I said: This thing that you do is not good [(before the L-rd)]. Would you not pursue the fear of G d in the face of the shame of [i.e., your being shamed by] the gentiles, our foes!


###### Nehemiah 5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:10)


וְגַם־אֲנִי֙ אַחַ֣י וּנְעָרַ֔י נֹשִׁ֥ים בָּהֶ֖ם כֶּ֣סֶף וְדָגָ֑ן נַֽעַזְבָה־נָּ֖א אֶת־הַמַּשָּׁ֥א הַזֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I likewise, and my brethren, and my servants, have lent them money and corn: I pray you, let us release this debt.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I, too, (and) my brethren and my youths are their creditors in money and in grain, and we shall now waive this debt [(and you follow our example!)]


###### Nehemiah 5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:11)


הָשִׁ֩יבוּ֩ נָ֨א לָהֶ֜ם כְּהַיּ֗וֹם שְׂדֹתֵיהֶ֛ם כַּרְמֵיהֶ֥ם זֵיתֵיהֶ֖ם וּבָתֵּיהֶ֑ם וּמְאַ֨ת הַכֶּ֤סֶף וְהַדָּגָן֙ הַתִּיר֣וֹשׁ וְהַיִּצְהָ֔ר אֲשֶׁ֥ר אַתֶּ֖ם נֹשִׁ֥ים בָּהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Restore, I pray you, to them, even this day, their lands, their vineyards, their oliveyards, and their houses, also the percentage of the money, and of the corn, the wine, and the oil, that you exact of them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Return to them, now, as of this day, their fields, their vineyards, their olive orchards, and their houses, and most of the money and the grain and the wine and the oil wherein you are their creditors.


###### Nehemiah 5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:12)


וַיֹּאמְר֣וּ נָשִׁ֗יב וּמֵהֶם֙ לֹ֣א נְבַקֵּ֔שׁ כֵּ֣ן נַעֲשֶׂ֔ה כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר אַתָּ֣ה אוֹמֵ֑ר וָאֶקְרָא֙ אֶת־הַכֹּ֣הֲנִ֔ים וָֽאַשְׁבִּיעֵ֔ם לַעֲשׂ֖וֹת כַּדָּבָ֥ר הַזֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then they said, We will restore them, and will require nothing of them; so will we do as thou sayst. Then I called the priests, and took an oath of them, that they should do according to this promise.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they said: We shall return (it) and we shall ask nothing of them. We shall do exactly as you have said. And I called the priests and I beswore them to do as this thing [i.e., to waive their loans].


###### Nehemiah 5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:13)


גַּם־חׇצְנִ֣י נָעַ֗רְתִּי וָֽאֹמְרָ֡ה כָּ֣כָה יְנַעֵ֪ר הָֽאֱלֹהִ֟ים אֶת־כׇּל־הָאִישׁ֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר לֹֽא־יָקִ֜ים אֶת־הַדָּבָ֣ר הַזֶּ֗ה מִבֵּיתוֹ֙ וּמִ֣יגִיע֔וֹ וְכָ֛כָה יִהְיֶ֥ה נָע֖וּר וָרֵ֑ק וַיֹּאמְר֨וּ כׇֽל־הַקָּהָ֜ל אָמֵ֗ן וַֽיְהַלְלוּ֙ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֔ה וַיַּ֥עַשׂ הָעָ֖ם כַּדָּבָ֥ר הַזֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Also I shook out my lap, and said, So God shake out every man from his house, and from his labour, who does not perform this promise, even thus may he be shaken out, and emptied. And all the congregation said, Amen, and praised the Lord. And the people did according to this promise.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I also shook out my hem and said: So shall G d shake out every man who does not fulfill this thing, of his house and of his toil [i.e., his possessions], and thus shall he remain shaken out and empty. And all the congregation said "Amen!" and they praised the L-rd, and the people did according to this thing [i.e., as they had averred.]


###### Nehemiah 5:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:14)


גַּ֞ם מִיּ֣וֹם**׀** אֲשֶׁר־צִוָּ֣ה אוֹתִ֗י לִהְי֣וֹת פֶּחָם֮ בְּאֶ֣רֶץ יְהוּדָה֒ מִשְּׁנַ֣ת עֶשְׂרִ֗ים וְ֠עַ֠ד שְׁנַ֨ת שְׁלֹשִׁ֤ים וּשְׁתַּ֙יִם֙ לְאַרְתַּחְשַׁ֣סְתְּא הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ שָׁנִ֖ים שְׁתֵּ֣ים עֶשְׂרֵ֑ה אֲנִ֣י וְאַחַ֔י לֶ֥חֶם הַפֶּחָ֖ה לֹ֥א אָכַֽלְתִּי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover from the time that I was appointed to be their governor in the land of Yehuda, from the twentieth year even to the thirty second year of Artaĥshasta the king, that is, twelve years, I and my brethren have not eaten the bread of the governor.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Also, from the day that he [(the king)] charged me to be governor of the land of Judah, from the twentieth year [(of the reign of King Darius, at which time Nechemiah went up from Bavel [viz. chapter 2])] until the thirty-second year of Artachshast [Darius] the king — twelve years — I and my brethren have not eaten the governor's bread [(a perquisite of the collection of taxes for the king)].


###### Nehemiah 5:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:15)


וְהַפַּחוֹת֩ הָרִאשֹׁנִ֨ים אֲשֶׁר־לְפָנַ֜י הִכְבִּ֣ידוּ עַל־הָעָ֗ם וַיִּקְח֨וּ מֵהֶ֜ם בְּלֶ֤חֶם וָיַ֙יִן֙ אַחַר֙ כֶּֽסֶף־שְׁקָלִ֣ים אַרְבָּעִ֔ים גַּ֥ם נַעֲרֵיהֶ֖ם שָׁלְט֣וּ עַל־הָעָ֑ם וַאֲנִי֙ לֹא־עָשִׂ֣יתִי כֵ֔ן מִפְּנֵ֖י יִרְאַ֥ת אֱלֹהִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the former governors who had been before me laid burdens upon the people, and had taken of them for bread and wine, besides forty shekels of silver; even their servants bore rule over the people: but I did not do so, because of the fear of God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the governors before me had imposed heavy [taxes] upon the people and had taken from them [for this service to the king] bread and wine, on top of forty silver shekels. And their youths, too, had lorded it over the people. But I did not do so, out of fear of G-d.


###### Nehemiah 5:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:16)


וְ֠גַ֠ם בִּמְלֶ֜אכֶת הַחוֹמָ֤ה הַזֹּאת֙ הֶחֱזַ֔קְתִּי וְשָׂדֶ֖ה לֹ֣א קָנִ֑ינוּ וְכׇ֨ל־נְעָרַ֔י קְבוּצִ֥ים שָׁ֖ם עַל־הַמְּלָאכָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For I also continued in the work of this wall, nor did we buy any land: and all my servants were gathered there to the work.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I also was [constantly] preoccupied with the work of this wall, and we [(I and my co-workers)] did not buy a field [(of the Jews building the wall wherewith to compensate them for their labors, but I paid them from my own funds)], and all of my youths were gathered there over the work.


###### Nehemiah 5:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:17)


וְהַיְּהוּדִ֨ים וְהַסְּגָנִ֜ים מֵאָ֧ה וַחֲמִשִּׁ֣ים אִ֗ישׁ וְהַבָּאִ֥ים אֵלֵ֛ינוּ מִן־הַגּוֹיִ֥ם אֲשֶׁר־סְבִיבֹתֵ֖ינוּ עַל־שֻׁלְחָנִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover there were at my table a hundred and fifty of the Jews and the rulers, besides those who came to us from among the nations who were round about us.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And, at my table were the Jews [(of the people)] and the officers, a hundred and fifty men, (aside from those [i.e., the proselytes] who came to us from the nations around us.)


###### Nehemiah 5:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:18)


וַאֲשֶׁר֩ הָיָ֨ה נַעֲשֶׂ֜ה לְי֣וֹם אֶחָ֗ד שׁ֣וֹר אֶחָ֞ד צֹ֠אן שֵׁשׁ־בְּרֻר֤וֹת וְצִפֳּרִים֙ נַֽעֲשׂוּ־לִ֔י וּבֵ֨ין עֲשֶׂ֧רֶת יָמִ֛ים בְּכׇל־יַ֖יִן לְהַרְבֵּ֑ה וְעִם־זֶ֗ה לֶ֤חֶם הַפֶּחָה֙ לֹ֣א בִקַּ֔שְׁתִּי כִּֽי־כָֽבְדָ֥ה הָעֲבֹדָ֖ה עַל־הָעָ֥ם הַזֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now that which was prepared for one day was one ox and six choice sheep; also fowls were prepared for me, and once in ten days store of all sorts of wine: yet for all this I did not demand the bread of the governor, because the bondage was heavy upon this people.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And, one day's fare was one ox (and) five stuffed sheep; and [an abundance of] fowl was prepared for me. And [every day we drank] enough wine for ten days. And, notwithstanding all this [i.e., all these expenses], I did not request [(from the people)] the governor's bread, for the work [of the wall] was heavy on this people.


###### Nehemiah 5:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/5:19)


זׇכְרָה־לִּ֥י אֱלֹהַ֖י לְטוֹבָ֑ה כֹּ֥ל אֲשֶׁר־עָשִׂ֖יתִי עַל־הָעָ֥ם הַזֶּֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Remember me, my God, for good, according to all that I have done for this people.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Remember unto me, my G-d, for good, all that I have done with this people.



## Chapter 6

###### Nehemiah 6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:1)


וַיְהִ֣י כַאֲשֶׁ֣ר נִשְׁמַ֣ע לְסַנְבַלַּ֣ט וְ֠טוֹבִיָּ֠ה וּלְגֶ֨שֶׁם הָֽעַרְבִ֜י וּלְיֶ֣תֶר אֹֽיְבֵ֗ינוּ כִּ֤י בָנִ֙יתִי֙ אֶת־הַ֣חוֹמָ֔ה וְלֹא־נ֥וֹתַר בָּ֖הּ פָּ֑רֶץ גַּ֚ם עַד־הָעֵ֣ת הַהִ֔יא דְּלָת֖וֹת לֹא־הֶעֱמַ֥דְתִּי בַשְּׁעָרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now it came to pass, when Sanvallat, and Toviyya, and Geshem the ῾Arvian, and the rest of our enemies, heard that I had built the wall, and that there was no breach left in it; (though at that time I had not set up the doors upon the gates;)


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, when it was heard by Sanvalat and Toviah and Geshem the Arab and the rest of our foes that I had built the wall and no breach remained in it, (and) also, (that) up to that time I had not set doors in the gates [(viz. verse 10)],


###### Nehemiah 6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:2)


וַיִּשְׁלַ֨ח סַנְבַלַּ֤ט וְגֶ֙שֶׁם֙ אֵלַ֣י לֵאמֹ֔ר לְכָ֞ה וְנִֽוָּעֲדָ֥ה יַחְדָּ֛ו בַּכְּפִירִ֖ים בְּבִקְעַ֣ת אוֹנ֑וֹ וְהֵ֙מָּה֙ חֹֽשְׁבִ֔ים לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת לִ֖י רָעָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then Sanvallat and Geshem sent to me, saying, Come, let us meet together in one of the villages in the plain of Ono. But they thought to do me mischief.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
that Sanvalat and Geshem sent to me, saying: Go, and we will meet together in Kefirim in the valley of Ono (and [I understood that] they were plotting to do me evil [i.e., to kill me])


###### Nehemiah 6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:3)


וָאֶשְׁלְחָ֨ה עֲלֵיהֶ֤ם מַלְאָכִים֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר מְלָאכָ֤ה גְדוֹלָה֙ אֲנִ֣י עֹשֶׂ֔ה וְלֹ֥א אוּכַ֖ל לָרֶ֑דֶת לָ֣מָּה תִשְׁבַּ֤ת הַמְּלָאכָה֙ כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר אַרְפֶּ֔הָ וְיָרַדְתִּ֖י אֲלֵיכֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I sent messengers to them, saying, I am doing a great work, so that I cannot come down: why should the work cease, whilst I leave it, and come down to you?


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I sent to them messengers, saying: I am busy with a great project and I will not be able to come down. Why should the work halt? When I complete it, I will come down to you.


###### Nehemiah 6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:4)


וַיִּשְׁלְח֥וּ אֵלַ֛י כַּדָּבָ֥ר הַזֶּ֖ה אַרְבַּ֣ע פְּעָמִ֑ים וָאָשִׁ֥יב אוֹתָ֖ם כַּדָּבָ֥ר הַזֶּֽה׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So they sent to me four times after this sort; and I answered them after the same manner.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they sent me in this wise four times, and I returned answer in this wise [i.e., as I had before].


###### Nehemiah 6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:5)


וַיִּשְׁלַח֩ אֵלַ֨י סַנְבַלַּ֜ט כַּדָּבָ֥ר הַזֶּ֛ה פַּ֥עַם חֲמִישִׁ֖ית אֶֽת־נַעֲר֑וֹ וְאִגֶּ֥רֶת פְּתוּחָ֖ה בְּיָדֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Sanvallat sent his servant to me in like manner the fifth time with an open letter in his hand:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Sanvalat sent me in this wise a fifth time by his youth with an open [i.e., an unsealed] letter in his hand.


###### Nehemiah 6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:6)


כָּת֣וּב בָּ֗הּ בַּגּוֹיִ֤ם נִשְׁמָע֙ וְגַשְׁמ֣וּ אֹמֵ֔ר אַתָּ֤ה וְהַיְּהוּדִים֙ חֹשְׁבִ֣ים לִמְר֔וֹד עַל־כֵּ֛ן אַתָּ֥ה בוֹנֶ֖ה הַחוֹמָ֑ה וְאַתָּ֗ה הֹוֶ֤ה לָהֶם֙ לְמֶ֔לֶךְ כַּדְּבָרִ֖ים הָאֵֽלֶּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
in which was written, It is reported among the nations, and Gashmu says it, that thou and the Jews think to rebel: for which cause thou didst build the wall, that thou mayst be their king, according to these words.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In it, such words were written: It has been heard [(by all the gentiles)] and Gashmu [the Arab also] says: You and the Jews are plotting to rebel, wherefore you are building the wall, and you would be their king.


###### Nehemiah 6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:7)


וְגַם־נְבִיאִ֡ים הֶעֱמַ֣דְתָּ לִקְרֹא֩ עָלֶ֨יךָ בִֽירוּשָׁלַ֜͏ִם לֵאמֹ֗ר מֶ֚לֶךְ בִּֽיהוּדָ֔ה וְעַתָּה֙ יִשָּׁמַ֣ע לַמֶּ֔לֶךְ כַּדְּבָרִ֖ים הָאֵ֑לֶּה וְעַתָּ֣ה לְכָ֔ה וְנִֽוָּעֲצָ֖ה יַחְדָּֽו׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou hast also appointed prophets to proclaim of these at Yerushalayim, saying, There is a king in Yehuda: and now shall it be reported to the king according to these words. Come now therefore, and let us take counsel together.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you have also set up speakers to proclaim over you in Jerusalem: "There is a king in Judah!" And now, these things will be heard by the king. And now, come, and we will take counsel together.


###### Nehemiah 6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:8)


וָאֶשְׁלְחָ֤ה אֵלָיו֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר לֹ֤א נִֽהְיָה֙ כַּדְּבָרִ֣ים הָאֵ֔לֶּה אֲשֶׁ֖ר אַתָּ֣ה אוֹמֵ֑ר כִּ֥י מִֽלִּבְּךָ֖ אַתָּ֥ה בוֹדָֽאם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then I sent to him, saying, There are no such things done as thou sayst, but thou dost invent them out of thy own heart.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I sent to him, saying: These things that you speak of are not so; you are imagining them.


###### Nehemiah 6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:9)


כִּ֣י כֻלָּ֗ם מְיָֽרְאִ֤ים אוֹתָ֙נוּ֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר יִרְפּ֧וּ יְדֵיהֶ֛ם מִן־הַמְּלָאכָ֖ה וְלֹ֣א תֵעָשֶׂ֑ה וְעַתָּ֖ה חַזֵּ֥ק אֶת־יָדָֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For they all would have us afraid, saying, Their hands shall be weakened from the work, that it shall not be done. (Now therefore, strengthen my hands.)


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For all of them [(the gentiles)] fear us, wherefore they say: Let them leave off the work so that it not be done. And now, assist me [(in building the wall)].


###### Nehemiah 6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:10)


וַאֲנִי־בָ֗אתִי בֵּ֣ית שְֽׁמַֽעְיָ֧ה בֶן־דְּלָיָ֛ה בֶּן־מְהֵֽיטַבְאֵ֖ל וְה֣וּא עָצ֑וּר וַיֹּ֡אמֶר נִוָּעֵד֩ אֶל־בֵּ֨ית הָאֱלֹהִ֜ים אֶל־תּ֣וֹךְ הַֽהֵיכָ֗ל וְנִסְגְּרָה֙ דַּלְת֣וֹת הַהֵיכָ֔ל כִּ֚י בָּאִ֣ים לְהׇרְגֶ֔ךָ וְלַ֖יְלָה בָּאִ֥ים לְהׇרְגֶֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Afterwards I came to the house of Shema῾ya the son of Delaya the son of Mehetav᾽el, who was shut up; and he said, Let us meet together in the house of God, within the temple, and let us shut the doors of the temple: for they will come to slay thee; yea, in the night they will come to slay thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I came to the house of Shemayah the son of Delayah the son of Mehetavel [(a false prophet, hired by Sanvalat and his cohorts to frighten Nechemiah into halting the work on the wall)], and he was at home. And he said: Let us meet in the house of G-d, in the midst of the sanctuary, and let us lock the doors of the sanctuary, for they are coming to kill you [(by way of the city gates, which were still without doors, [viz. 1])]; and they are coming to kill you tonight.


###### Nehemiah 6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:11)


וָאֹמְרָ֗ה הַאִ֤ישׁ כָּמ֙וֹנִי֙ יִבְרָ֔ח וּמִ֥י כָמ֛וֹנִי אֲשֶׁר־יָבֹ֥א אֶל־הַהֵיכָ֖ל וָחָ֑י לֹ֖א אָבֽוֹא׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I said, Should such a man as I flee? and who such as I could go into the temple and live? I will not go in.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I said: Shall a man such as I run away [(out of fear)]? And who such as I [(a non-priest)] may come to the sanctuary and live? [viz. Numbers 3:38] I shall not come!


###### Nehemiah 6:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:12)


וָאַכִּ֕ירָה וְהִנֵּ֥ה לֹֽא־אֱלֹהִ֖ים שְׁלָח֑וֹ כִּ֤י הַנְּבוּאָה֙ דִּבֶּ֣ר עָלַ֔י וְטוֹבִיָּ֥ה וְסַנְבַלַּ֖ט שְׂכָרֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And, lo, I perceived that God had not sent him; but that he pronounced this prophecy against me: for Toviyya and Sanvallat had hired him.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I realized that, behold, not G-d had sent him (for he spoke [these things] to me [as being] prophecy) and (that) Toviah and Sanvalat had hired him.


###### Nehemiah 6:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:13)


לְמַ֤עַן שָׂכוּר֙ ה֔וּא לְמַֽעַן־אִירָ֥א וְאֶֽעֱשֶׂה־כֵּ֖ן וְחָטָ֑אתִי וְהָיָ֤ה לָהֶם֙ לְשֵׁ֣ם רָ֔ע לְמַ֖עַן יְחָֽרְפֽוּנִי׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For this cause was he hired, that I should be afraid, and do so, and sin, and that they might have matter for an evil report, that they might taunt me.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Because he was hired [(had he spoken thus to me)], so that I should fear and do this [i.e., enter the sanctuary] and sin and enable them to vilify me [(as a sinner)] and shame me.


###### Nehemiah 6:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:14)


זׇכְרָ֧ה אֱלֹהַ֛י לְטוֹבִיָּ֥ה וּלְסַנְבַלַּ֖ט כְּמַעֲשָׂ֣יו אֵ֑לֶּה וְגַ֨ם לְנוֹעַדְיָ֤ה הַנְּבִיאָה֙ וּלְיֶ֣תֶר הַנְּבִיאִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר הָי֖וּ מְיָֽרְאִ֥ים אוֹתִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My God, remember Toviyya and Sanvallat according to these their works, and also the prophetess No῾adya, and the rest of the prophets, who would have put me in fear.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Remember, my G-d, Toviah and Sanvalat [to punish them] for these deeds, as well as Noadiah the prophetess and the other prophets who would frighten me [for hire].


###### Nehemiah 6:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:15)


וַתִּשְׁלַם֙ הַֽחוֹמָ֔ה בְּעֶשְׂרִ֥ים וַחֲמִשָּׁ֖ה לֶאֱל֑וּל לַחֲמִשִּׁ֥ים וּשְׁנַ֖יִם יֽוֹם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So the wall was finished in the twenty fifth day of the month Elul, in fifty two days.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the wall was completed on the twenty-fifth of Elul [(in the twentieth year of King Darius, when Nechemiah had come up from Bavel)], on the fifty-second day [(after building had begun)].


###### Nehemiah 6:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:16)


וַיְהִ֗י כַּאֲשֶׁ֤ר שָֽׁמְעוּ֙ כׇּל־א֣וֹיְבֵ֔ינוּ וַיִּֽרְא֗וּ כׇּל־הַגּוֹיִם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר סְבִֽיבֹתֵ֔ינוּ וַיִּפְּל֥וּ מְאֹ֖ד בְּעֵינֵיהֶ֑ם וַיֵּ֣דְע֔וּ כִּ֚י מֵאֵ֣ת אֱלֹהֵ֔ינוּ נֶעֶשְׂתָ֖ה הַמְּלָאכָ֥ה הַזֹּֽאת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass, that when all our enemies heard that, all the nations that were about us were afraid and were much cast down in their own eyes: for they perceived that this work was done by our God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when all of our foes heard and all the nations around us saw [(that the city wall had been completed)], they fell greatly in their (own) eyes, [(the fear of the Jews falling upon them)], and they knew that it was from the L-rd that this work had been done.


###### Nehemiah 6:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:17)


גַּ֣ם**׀** בַּיָּמִ֣ים הָהֵ֗ם מַרְבִּ֞ים חֹרֵ֤י יְהוּדָה֙ אִגְּרֹ֣תֵיהֶ֔ם הוֹלְכ֖וֹת עַל־טוֹבִיָּ֑ה וַאֲשֶׁ֥ר לְטוֹבִיָּ֖ה בָּא֥וֹת אֲלֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover in those days the nobles of Yehuda sent many letters to Toviyya, and the letters of Toviyya came to them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Even in those days the nobles of Judah sent letters frequently to Toviah [(a wicked Jew)], and those of Toviah came to them.


###### Nehemiah 6:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:18)


כִּי־רַבִּ֣ים בִּֽיהוּדָ֗ה בַּעֲלֵ֤י שְׁבוּעָה֙ ל֔וֹ כִּי־חָתָ֥ן ה֖וּא לִשְׁכַנְיָ֣ה בֶן־אָרַ֑ח וִֽיהוֹחָנָ֣ן בְּנ֔וֹ לָקַ֕ח אֶת־בַּת־מְשֻׁלָּ֖ם בֶּ֥ן בֶּֽרֶכְיָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For there were many in Yehuda sworn to him, because he was the son in law of Shekhanya the son of Araĥ; and his son Yehoĥanan had taken the daughter of Meshullam the son of Berekhya.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For many in Judah were covenanted with him. For he was a son-in-law of Shechanyah the son of Arach, and Jonathan his [(Toviah's)] son had wed the daughter of Meshulam the son of Berechyah.


###### Nehemiah 6:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/6:19)


גַּ֣ם טוֹבֹתָ֗יו הָי֤וּ אֹמְרִים֙ לְפָנַ֔י וּדְבָרַ֕י הָי֥וּ מוֹצִיאִ֖ים ל֑וֹ אִגְּר֛וֹת שָׁלַ֥ח טוֹבִיָּ֖ה לְיָֽרְאֵֽנִי׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Also they spoke of his good deeds before me, and reported my words to him. And Toviyya sent letters to put me in fear.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### Nehemiah 7:1

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### Nehemiah 7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:2)


וָאֲצַוֶּ֞ה אֶת־חֲנָ֣נִי אָחִ֗י וְאֶת־חֲנַנְיָ֛ה שַׂ֥ר הַבִּירָ֖ה עַל־יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם כִּי־הוּא֙ כְּאִ֣ישׁ אֱמֶ֔ת וְיָרֵ֥א אֶת־הָאֱלֹהִ֖ים מֵרַבִּֽים׃

that I gave my brother Ḥanani, and Ḥananya the ruler of the palace, charge over Yerushalayim: for he was a faithful man, and feared God above many.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
that I charged Chanani my brother and Chananiah, chief of the capital with [the guarding of the gates of] Jerusalem, for he was a man of truth and feared G-d of yore.


###### Nehemiah 7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:3)


(ויאמר) [וָאֹמַ֣ר] לָהֶ֗ם לֹ֣א יִפָּֽתְח֞וּ שַׁעֲרֵ֤י יְרוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ עַד־חֹ֣ם הַשֶּׁ֔מֶשׁ וְעַ֨ד הֵ֥ם עֹמְדִ֛ים יָגִ֥יפוּ הַדְּלָת֖וֹת וֶאֱחֹ֑זוּ וְהַעֲמֵ֗יד מִשְׁמְרוֹת֙ יֹשְׁבֵ֣י יְרוּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם אִ֚ישׁ בְּמִשְׁמָר֔וֹ וְאִ֖ישׁ נֶ֥גֶד בֵּיתֽוֹ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I said to them, Let not the gates of Yerushalayim be opened until the sun is hot; and while people are still standing there, let them shut the doors, and bar them: and appoint watches among the inhabitants of Yerushalayim everyone in his watch, and everyone to be over against his house.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I said to them: Let the gates of Jerusalem not be opened until the heat of the day [i.e., midday]. [(While the gates were open, they were to remain standing there, and whoever wished to leave or enter did so at that time.)] And while they were [yet] standing [there], they were to take hold of the doors to close (them). And [("I said to them":)] Station watches (of) the inhabitants of Jerusalem, each in his watch and each opposite his house.

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
