וְאֵ֗לֶּה הָֽעוֹלִים֙ מִתֵּ֥ל מֶ֙לַח֙ תֵּ֣ל חַרְשָׁ֔א כְּר֥וּב אַדּ֖וֹן וְאִמֵּ֑ר וְלֹ֣א יָכְל֗וּ לְהַגִּ֤יד בֵּית־אֲבֹתָם֙ וְזַרְעָ֔ם אִ֥ם מִיִּשְׂרָאֵ֖ל הֵֽם׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And these were they who went up from Tel-melaĥ, Tel-ĥarsha, Keruv, Addon, and Immer: but they could not tell their father’s houses, nor their seed, Whether they were of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And those who went up from Tel-melach, Tel-charshah, Keruv, Addon (and) Immer could not tell [the origin of] the house of their fathers or their seed [(having lost their genealogical records and not knowing)] whether they were Israelites.


###### Nehemiah 7:62

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:62](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:62)


בְּנֵי־דְלָיָ֥ה בְנֵֽי־טוֹבִיָּ֖ה בְּנֵ֣י נְקוֹדָ֑א שֵׁ֥שׁ מֵא֖וֹת וְאַרְבָּעִ֥ים וּשְׁנָֽיִם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The children of Delaya, the children of Toviyya, the children of Neqoda, six hundred and forty two.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(The above included)] the sons of Delayah, the sons of Toviyah (and) the sons of Nekodah — six hundred and forty-two.


###### Nehemiah 7:63

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:63](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:63)


וּמִן־הַכֹּ֣הֲנִ֔ים בְּנֵ֥י חֳבַיָּ֖ה בְּנֵ֣י הַקּ֑וֹץ בְּנֵ֣י בַרְזִלַּ֗י אֲשֶׁ֣ר לָ֠קַ֠ח מִבְּנ֞וֹת בַּרְזִלַּ֤י הַגִּלְעָדִי֙ אִשָּׁ֔ה וַיִּקָּרֵ֖א עַל־שְׁמָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And of the priests: the children of Ḥavaya, the children of Qoż, the children of Barzillay, who took one of the daughters of Barzillay the Gil῾adite to wife, and was called after their name.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And of the priests, the sons of Chavayah, the sons of Hakotz, the sons of Barzilai [each of whose sons] took of the daughters of Barzilai as a wife, and whose [(Barzilai's)] name was called upon them —


###### Nehemiah 7:64

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:64](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:64)


אֵ֗לֶּה בִּקְשׁ֧וּ כְתָבָ֛ם הַמִּתְיַחְשִׂ֖ים וְלֹ֣א נִמְצָ֑א וַיְגֹֽאֲל֖וּ מִן־הַכְּהֻנָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These sought their register, that is, the genealogy, but it was not found: therefore they were excluded from the priesthood as unfit.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
these [priests], who would trace themselves [to the priesthood] sought their [genealogical] records, and they could not be found, and they were rejected from the priesthood.


###### Nehemiah 7:65

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:65](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:65)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר הַתִּרְשָׁ֙תָא֙ לָהֶ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־יֹאכְל֖וּ מִקֹּ֣דֶשׁ הַקֳּדָשִׁ֑ים עַ֛ד עֲמֹ֥ד הַכֹּהֵ֖ן לְאוּרִ֥ים וְתֻמִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Tirshata said to them, that they should not eat of the most holy things, until there stood up a priest with Urim and Tummim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Hatirshatha [(Nechemiah the son of Chachalyah)] told them that they should not [(as other priests)] eat of the holy of holies [(sin-offerings and guilt-offerings)] until a priest should arise for the urim vetumim [(to all intents and purposes, "until the days of the Messiah")].


###### Nehemiah 7:66

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:66](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:66)


כׇּל־הַקָּהָ֖ל כְּאֶחָ֑ד אַרְבַּ֣ע רִבּ֔וֹא אַלְפַּ֖יִם שְׁלֹשׁ־מֵא֥וֹת וְשִׁשִּֽׁים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The whole congregation together was forty two thousand three hundred and sixty,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All the congregation together [(including the priests, the Levites, the Nethinim and the other tribes — those counted above being only those of Judah and Benjamin —)] were forty-two thousand and three hundred and sixty.


###### Nehemiah 7:67

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:67](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:67)


מִ֠לְּבַ֠ד עַבְדֵיהֶ֤ם וְאַמְהֹֽתֵיהֶם֙ אֵ֔לֶּה שִׁבְעַ֣ת אֲלָפִ֔ים שְׁלֹ֥שׁ מֵא֖וֹת שְׁלֹשִׁ֣ים וְשִׁבְעָ֑ה וְלָהֶ֗ם מְשֹֽׁרְרִים֙ וּמְשֹׁ֣רְר֔וֹת מָאתַ֖יִם וְאַרְבָּעִ֥ים וַחֲמִשָּֽׁה׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
besides their men servants and their women servants, of whom there were seven thousand three hundred and thirty seven: and they had two hundred and forty five singing men and singing women. [This verse is inserted in some versions: Their horses, seven hundred and thirty six: their mules, two hundred and forty five: ]


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Aside from their men-servants and their maid-servants, who numbered seven thousand and three hundred and thirty-seven. And with them were men-singers and women-singers, two hundred and forty-five [(to bring them back to Eretz Yisrael with joy)].


###### Nehemiah 7:68

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:68](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:68)


גְּמַלִּ֕ים אַרְבַּ֥ע מֵא֖וֹת שְׁלֹשִׁ֣ים וַחֲמִשָּׁ֑ה חֲמֹרִ֕ים שֵׁ֣שֶׁת אֲלָפִ֔ים שְׁבַ֥ע מֵא֖וֹת וְעֶשְׂרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
their camels, four hundred and thirty five: six thousand seven hundred and twenty asses:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
(Their horses, seven hundred and thirty-six. Their mules, two hundred and forty-five.) Their camels, four hundred and thirty-five. Asses, six thousand and seven hundred and twenty.


###### Nehemiah 7:69

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:69](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:69)


וּמִקְצָת֙ רָאשֵׁ֣י הָֽאָב֔וֹת נָתְנ֖וּ לַמְּלָאכָ֑ה הַתִּרְשָׁ֜תָא נָתַ֣ן לָאוֹצָ֗ר זָהָ֞ב דַּרְכְּמֹנִ֥ים אֶ֙לֶף֙ מִזְרָק֣וֹת חֲמִשִּׁ֔ים כׇּתְנוֹת֙ כֹּֽהֲנִ֔ים שְׁלֹשִׁ֖ים וַחֲמֵ֥שׁ מֵאֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and some of the chief of the fathers’ houses gave for the work. The Tirshata gave to the treasury a thousand drachmas of gold, fifty basins, five hundred and thirty priests’ garments.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And some of the heads of the fathers' houses contributed to the work [viz. Ezra 2:68-69]. Hatirshatha [(Nechemiah)] gave to the [Temple] treasury one thousand golden darkemonim, fifty basins, and three hundred and fifty priestly tunics [(in which to minister)].


###### Nehemiah 7:70

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:70](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:70)


וּמֵֽרָאשֵׁ֣י הָֽאָב֗וֹת נָֽתְנוּ֙ לְאוֹצַ֣ר הַמְּלָאכָ֔ה זָהָ֕ב דַּרְכְּמוֹנִ֖ים שְׁתֵּ֣י רִבּ֑וֹת וְכֶ֕סֶף מָנִ֖ים אַלְפַּ֥יִם וּמָאתָֽיִם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And some of the chiefs of the fathers’ houses gave to the treasury of the work twenty thousand drachmas of gold, and two thousand and two hundred minas of silver.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And some of the heads of the fathers' houses gave to the building fund twenty thousand golden darkemonim and two thousand and five hundred silver manim.


###### Nehemiah 7:71

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:71](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:71)


וַאֲשֶׁ֣ר נָתְנוּ֮ שְׁאֵרִ֣ית הָעָם֒ זָהָ֗ב דַּרְכְּמֹנִים֙ שְׁתֵּ֣י רִבּ֔וֹא וְכֶ֖סֶף מָנִ֣ים אַלְפָּ֑יִם וְכׇתְנֹ֥ת כֹּֽהֲנִ֖ים שִׁשִּׁ֥ים וְשִׁבְעָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And that which the rest of the people gave was twenty thousand drachmas of gold, and two thousand minas of silver, and sixty seven priests’ garments.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And what the rest of the people gave — twenty thousand golden darkemonim, two thousand silver manim, and sixty-seven priestly tunics.


###### Nehemiah 7:72

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:72](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/7:72)


וַיֵּשְׁב֣וּ הַכֹּהֲנִ֣ים וְהַלְוִיִּ֡ם וְהַשּׁוֹעֲרִים֩ וְהַמְשֹׁרְרִ֨ים וּמִן־הָעָ֧ם וְהַנְּתִינִ֛ים וְכׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל בְּעָרֵיהֶ֑ם{ס}וַיִּגַּע֙ הַחֹ֣דֶשׁ הַשְּׁבִיעִ֔י וּבְנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל בְּעָרֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So the priests, and the Levites, and the gatekeepers, and the singers, and some of the people, and the temple servants, and all Yisra᾽el, dwelt in their cities; and when the seventh month came, and the children of Yisra᾽el were in their cities,


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the priests and the Levites and the gatekeepers and the singers and some of the people [(those of Judah and Benjamin)] and the Nethinim and all of Israel [(those of the other tribes)] dwelt in their cities [(i.e., what had been the cities of their inheritance before they were exiled)]. And the seventh month arrived and the children of Israel were in their cities.



## Chapter 8

###### Nehemiah 8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:1)


וַיֵּאָסְפ֤וּ כׇל־הָעָם֙ כְּאִ֣ישׁ אֶחָ֔ד אֶל־הָ֣רְח֔וֹב אֲשֶׁ֖ר לִפְנֵ֣י שַֽׁעַר־הַמָּ֑יִם וַיֹּֽאמְרוּ֙ לְעֶזְרָ֣א הַסֹּפֵ֔ר לְהָבִ֗יא אֶת־סֵ֙פֶר֙ תּוֹרַ֣ת מֹשֶׁ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־צִוָּ֥ה יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then all the people gathered themselves together as one man into the open place that was before the water gate; and they spoke to ῾Ezra the scribe to bring the book of the Tora of Moshe, which the Lord had commanded to Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the people gathered as one man to the square before the Water Gate, and they asked Ezra the scribe to bring the scroll of the Torah of Moses which the L-rd had commanded to Israel.


###### Nehemiah 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:2)


וַיָּבִ֣יא עֶזְרָ֣א הַ֠כֹּהֵ֠ן אֶֽת־הַתּוֹרָ֞ה לִפְנֵ֤י הַקָּהָל֙ מֵאִ֣ישׁ וְעַד־אִשָּׁ֔ה וְכֹ֖ל מֵבִ֣ין לִשְׁמֹ֑עַ בְּי֥וֹם אֶחָ֖ד לַחֹ֥דֶשׁ הַשְּׁבִיעִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And ῾Ezra the priest brought the Tora before the congregation both of men and women, and all those who could hear with understanding, upon the first day of the seventh month.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Ezra the priest brought the Torah before the congregation, from man to woman and all who could hear with understanding, on the first day of the seventh month [i.e., Rosh Hashanah].


###### Nehemiah 8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:3)


וַיִּקְרָא־בוֹ֩ לִפְנֵ֨י הָרְח֜וֹב אֲשֶׁ֣ר**׀** לִפְנֵ֣י שַֽׁעַר־הַמַּ֗יִם מִן־הָאוֹר֙ עַד־מַחֲצִ֣ית הַיּ֔וֹם נֶ֛גֶד הָאֲנָשִׁ֥ים וְהַנָּשִׁ֖ים וְהַמְּבִינִ֑ים וְאׇזְנֵ֥י כׇל־הָעָ֖ם אֶל־סֵ֥פֶר הַתּוֹרָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he read therein facing the open place which was before the water gate from morning until midday, before the men and the women, and those who could understand; and the ears of all the people were attentive to the book of the Tora.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he read in it before the square in front of the Water Gate from daylight until midday before the men, the women, and (the others) who understood. And the ears of all the people were to the Torah scroll.


###### Nehemiah 8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:4)


וַֽיַּעֲמֹ֞ד עֶזְרָ֣א הַסֹּפֵ֗ר עַֽל־מִגְדַּל־עֵץ֮ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשׂ֣וּ לַדָּבָר֒ וַיַּֽעֲמֹ֣ד אֶצְל֡וֹ מַתִּתְיָ֡ה וְשֶׁ֡מַע וַ֠עֲנָיָ֠ה וְאוּרִיָּ֧ה וְחִלְקִיָּ֛ה וּמַעֲשֵׂיָ֖ה עַל־יְמִינ֑וֹ וּמִשְּׂמֹאל֗וֹ פְּ֠דָיָ֠ה וּמִֽישָׁאֵ֧ל וּמַלְכִּיָּ֛ה וְחָשֻׁ֥ם וְחַשְׁבַּדָּ֖נָה זְכַרְיָ֥ה מְשֻׁלָּֽם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And ῾Ezra the scribe stood upon a platform of wood, which they had made for the purpose, and beside him stood Mattitya, and Shema, and ῾Anaya, and Uriyya, and Ḥilqiyya, and Ma῾aseya, on his right hand; and on his left hand, Pedaya and Misha᾽el, and Malkiyya, and Ḥashum, and Ḥashbaddana, Zekhrya, Meshullam.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Ezra stood on a wooden platform that they had made for the thing [(the reading of the Torah scroll)]. And there stood next to him: Matithyah, Shema, Anayah, Uriah, Chilkiyah, and Ma'aseyah, on his right; and, on his left, Pedayah, Mishael, Malkiyah, Chusham, Chashbadnah, Zecharyah, and Meshulam.


###### Nehemiah 8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:5)


וַיִּפְתַּ֨ח עֶזְרָ֤א הַסֵּ֙פֶר֙ לְעֵינֵ֣י כׇל־הָעָ֔ם כִּֽי־מֵעַ֥ל כׇּל־הָעָ֖ם הָיָ֑ה וּכְפִתְח֖וֹ עָֽמְד֥וּ כׇל־הָעָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And ῾Ezra opened the book in the sight of all the people; (for he was above all the people;) and when he opened it, all the people stood up:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Ezra opened the scroll in the sight of all of the people (for he was [standing] above all of the people). And when he opened it, all of the people stood silent.


###### Nehemiah 8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:6)


וַיְבָ֣רֶךְ עֶזְרָ֔א אֶת־יְהֹוָ֥ה הָאֱלֹהִ֖ים הַגָּד֑וֹל וַיַּֽעֲנ֨וּ כׇל־הָעָ֜ם אָמֵ֤ן׀אָמֵן֙ בְּמֹ֣עַל יְדֵיהֶ֔ם וַיִּקְּד֧וּ וַיִּֽשְׁתַּחֲו֛וּ לַיהֹוָ֖ה אַפַּ֥יִם אָֽרְצָה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and ῾Ezra blessed the Lord, the great God. And all the people answered, Amen, Amen, lifting up their hands: and they bowed their heads, and worshipped the Lord with their faces to the ground.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Ezra blessed the L-rd, the great G d, and all the people answered Amen! Amen! with hands uplifted [in praise of the L-rd]. And they bowed down and prostrated themselves to the L-rd with their faces to the ground.


###### Nehemiah 8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:7)


וְיֵשׁ֡וּעַ וּבָנִ֡י וְשֵׁרֵ֥בְיָ֣ה**׀** יָמִ֡ין עַקּ֡וּב שַׁבְּתַ֣י׀הֽוֹדִיָּ֡ה מַעֲשֵׂיָ֡ה קְלִיטָ֣א עֲזַרְיָה֩ יוֹזָבָ֨ד חָנָ֤ן פְּלָאיָה֙ וְהַלְוִיִּ֔ם מְבִינִ֥ים אֶת־הָעָ֖ם לַתּוֹרָ֑ה וְהָעָ֖ם עַל־עׇמְדָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Also Yeshua, and Bani, and Sherevya, Yamin, ῾Aqquv, Shabbetay, Hodiyya, Ma῾aseya, Qelita, ῾Azarya, Yozaved, Ḥanan, Pelaya, and the Levites, caused the people to understand the Tora: and the people stood in their place.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yeshua, Bani, Sherevyah, Yamin, Akuv, Shabthai, Hodiyah, Ma'aseyah, Kelita, Azaryah, Yozavad, Chanan, Pelayah, and the Levites explained the Torah to the people, the people standing.


###### Nehemiah 8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:8)


וַֽיִּקְרְא֥וּ בַסֵּ֛פֶר בְּתוֹרַ֥ת הָאֱלֹהִ֖ים מְפֹרָ֑שׁ וְשׂ֣וֹם שֶׂ֔כֶל וַיָּבִ֖ינוּ בַּמִּקְרָֽא׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So they read in the book, in the Tora of God distinctly, and gave the sense, and caused them to understand the reading.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they read in the explicated scroll of the Torah of G-d with application, and they understood what was written.


###### Nehemiah 8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:9)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר נְחֶמְיָ֣ה ה֣וּא הַתִּרְשָׁ֡תָא וְעֶזְרָ֣א הַכֹּהֵ֣ן׀הַסֹּפֵ֡ר וְהַלְוִיִּם֩ הַמְּבִינִ֨ים אֶת־הָעָ֜ם לְכׇל־הָעָ֗ם הַיּ֤וֹם קָדֹֽשׁ־הוּא֙ לַיהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֔ם אַל־תִּֽתְאַבְּל֖וּ וְאַל־תִּבְכּ֑וּ כִּ֤י בוֹכִים֙ כׇּל־הָעָ֔ם כְּשׇׁמְעָ֖ם אֶת־דִּבְרֵ֥י הַתּוֹרָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Neĥemya, who is the Tirshata, and ῾Ezra the priest the scribe, and the Levites who taught the people, said to all the people, This day is holy to the Lord your God; do not mourn, or weep. For all the people wept, when they heard the words of the Tora.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Nechemiah (Tirshatha) and Ezra the priest, the scribe, and the Levites, the edifiers of the people, said to all the people: This day is holy to the L-rd your G d; do not mourn and do not weep. For all the people wept [(for their remissness in fulfilling the Torah)] when they heard the words of the Torah.


###### Nehemiah 8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:10)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר לָהֶ֡ם לְכוּ֩ אִכְל֨וּ מַשְׁמַנִּ֜ים וּשְׁת֣וּ מַֽמְתַקִּ֗ים וְשִׁלְח֤וּ מָנוֹת֙ לְאֵ֣ין נָכ֣וֹן ל֔וֹ כִּֽי־קָד֥וֹשׁ הַיּ֖וֹם לַאֲדֹנֵ֑ינוּ וְאַל־תֵּ֣עָצֵ֔בוּ כִּֽי־חֶדְוַ֥ת יְהֹוָ֖ה הִ֥יא מָֽעֻזְּכֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then he said to them, Go your way, eat sumptuously, and drink sweet beverages, and send portions to those for whom nothing is prepared: for this day is holy to our Lord: for the joy of the Lord is your strength.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said to them: Go, eat fat things and drink sweet things and send portions to him [i.e., to the poor one] who has no [food] readied. For this day is holy to our L-rd. And do not be sad, for the L-rd's joy is your stronghold.


###### Nehemiah 8:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:11)


וְהַלְוִיִּ֞ם מַחְשִׁ֤ים לְכׇל־הָעָם֙ לֵאמֹ֣ר הַ֔סּוּ כִּ֥י הַיּ֖וֹם קָדֹ֑שׁ וְאַל־תֵּעָצֵֽבוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So the Levites stilled all the people, saying, Hold your peace, for the day is holy; and do not be grieved.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Levites hushed all the people, saying: Be still, for this is a holy day; do not be sad.


###### Nehemiah 8:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:12)


וַיֵּלְכ֨וּ כׇל־הָעָ֜ם לֶאֱכֹ֤ל וְלִשְׁתּוֹת֙ וּלְשַׁלַּ֣ח מָנ֔וֹת וְלַעֲשׂ֖וֹת שִׂמְחָ֣ה גְדוֹלָ֑ה כִּ֤י הֵבִ֙ינוּ֙ בַּדְּבָרִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר הוֹדִ֖יעוּ לָהֶֽם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And all the people went their way to eat, and to drink, and to send portions, and to make great mirth, because they had understood the words that were declared to them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the people went to eat and to drink and to send portions and to make great festivity, for they understood the things whereof they apprised them.


###### Nehemiah 8:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:13)


וּבַיּ֣וֹם הַשֵּׁנִ֡י נֶאֶסְפוּ֩ רָאשֵׁ֨י הָאָב֜וֹת לְכׇל־הָעָ֗ם הַכֹּֽהֲנִים֙ וְהַלְוִיִּ֔ם אֶל־עֶזְרָ֖א הַסֹּפֵ֑ר וּלְהַשְׂכִּ֖יל אֶל־דִּבְרֵ֥י הַתּוֹרָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And on the second day, the chiefs of the fathers’ houses of all the people, the priests, and the Levites, were gathered to ῾Ezra the scribe, in order to study the words of the Tora.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And on the second day [(of Rosh Hashanah)] there gathered the heads of the fathers' [houses] of all the people (and) the priests and the Levites to Ezra the scribe to probe the words of the Torah.


###### Nehemiah 8:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:14)


וַֽיִּמְצְא֖וּ כָּת֣וּב בַּתּוֹרָ֑ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר צִוָּ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ בְּיַד־מֹשֶׁ֔ה אֲשֶׁר֩ יֵשְׁב֨וּ בְנֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֧ל בַּסֻּכּ֛וֹת בֶּחָ֖ג בַּחֹ֥דֶשׁ הַשְּׁבִיעִֽי׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they found written in the Tora which the Lord had commanded by the hand of Moshe, that the children of Yisra᾽el should dwell in booths in the feast of the seventh month:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they found it written in the Torah that the L-rd had commanded through Moses that the children of Israel were to dwell in succoth on the festival in the seventh month.


###### Nehemiah 8:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:15)


וַאֲשֶׁ֣ר יַשְׁמִ֗יעוּ וְיַעֲבִ֨ירוּ ק֥וֹל בְּכׇל־עָרֵיהֶם֮ וּבִירוּשָׁלַ֣͏ִם לֵאמֹר֒ צְא֣וּ הָהָ֗ר וְהָבִ֙יאוּ֙ עֲלֵי־זַ֙יִת֙ וַעֲלֵי־עֵ֣ץ שֶׁ֔מֶן וַעֲלֵ֤י הֲדַס֙ וַעֲלֵ֣י תְמָרִ֔ים וַעֲלֵ֖י עֵ֣ץ עָבֹ֑ת לַעֲשֹׂ֥ת סֻכֹּ֖ת כַּכָּתֽוּב׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and that they should publish and proclaim in all their cities, and in Yerushalayim, saying, Go out to the mountain, and fetch olive branches, and branches of wild olive, and myrtle branches, and palm branches, and branches of thick trees, to make booths, as it is written.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[And they commanded] that they make heard and proclaim throughout all of their cities and in Jerusalem: "Go out to the mountain and bring olive leaves and olive-tree leaves and myrtle leaves [(for roofing the succah)] and date-palm leaves [(for the lulav)] and leaves of a plaited tree [(hadassim)] to observe Succoth as written."


###### Nehemiah 8:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:16)


וַיֵּצְא֣וּ הָעָם֮ וַיָּבִ֒יאוּ֒ וַיַּעֲשׂוּ֩ לָהֶ֨ם סֻכּ֜וֹת אִ֤ישׁ עַל־גַּגּוֹ֙ וּבְחַצְרֹ֣תֵיהֶ֔ם וּבְחַצְר֖וֹת בֵּ֣ית הָאֱלֹהִ֑ים וּבִרְחוֹב֙ שַׁ֣עַר הַמַּ֔יִם וּבִרְח֖וֹב שַׁ֥עַר אֶפְרָֽיִם׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So the people went out, and brought them, and made themselves booths, every one upon the roof of his house, and in their courts, and in the courts of the house of God, and in the broad place of the water gate, and in the broad place of the gate of Efrayim.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the people went out and brought [(what they had been instructed to bring)], and they made succoth for themselves, each on his roof and in their courtyards and in the courts of the house of G-d and in the square of the Water Gate and in the square of the Gate of Ephraim.


###### Nehemiah 8:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:17)


וַיַּֽעֲשׂ֣וּ כׇֽל־הַ֠קָּהָ֠ל הַשָּׁבִ֨ים מִן־הַשְּׁבִ֥י׀סֻכּוֹת֮ וַיֵּשְׁב֣וּ בַסֻּכּוֹת֒ כִּ֣י לֹֽא־עָשׂ֡וּ מִימֵי֩ יֵשׁ֨וּעַ בִּן־נ֥וּן כֵּן֙ בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל עַ֖ד הַיּ֣וֹם הַה֑וּא וַתְּהִ֥י שִׂמְחָ֖ה גְּדוֹלָ֥ה מְאֹֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And all the congregation of those who were come back out of the captivity made booths, and dwelt in the booths: for since the days of Yeshua the son of Nun to that day the children of Yisra᾽el had not done so. And there was very great gladness.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the congregation that had returned from the captivity made succoth, and they dwelt in the succoth. For the children of Israel had not done so from the days of Yeshua the son of Nun until that day. And the joy was very great.


###### Nehemiah 8:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/8:18)


וַ֠יִּקְרָ֠א בְּסֵ֨פֶר תּוֹרַ֤ת הָאֱלֹהִים֙ י֣וֹם׀בְּי֔וֹם מִן־הַיּוֹם֙ הָֽרִאשׁ֔וֹן עַ֖ד הַיּ֣וֹם הָאַחֲר֑וֹן וַיַּֽעֲשׂוּ־חָג֙ שִׁבְעַ֣ת יָמִ֔ים וּבַיּ֧וֹם הַשְּׁמִינִ֛י עֲצֶ֖רֶת כַּמִּשְׁפָּֽט׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Also day by day, from the first day to the last day, he read in the book of the Tora of God. And they kept the feast seven days; and on the eighth day was a solemn assembly, according to the prescribed form.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he read in the scroll of the Torah of G-d every day [of the festival], from the first day until the last day. And they observed the festival for seven days, and on the eighth day, atzereth, as prescribed [viz. Leviticus 23:26].



## Chapter 9

###### Nehemiah 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:1)


וּבְיוֹם֩ עֶשְׂרִ֨ים וְאַרְבָּעָ֜ה לַחֹ֣דֶשׁ הַזֶּ֗ה נֶאֶסְפ֤וּ בְנֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ בְּצ֣וֹם וּבְשַׂקִּ֔ים וַאֲדָמָ֖ה עֲלֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now on the twenty fourth day of this month the children of Yisra᾽el were assembled with fasting, and with sackcloth, and with earth upon them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And on the twenty-fourth day of this month the children of Israel gathered together in fasting and in sackcloth and (with) earth upon them [i.e., upon their heads].


###### Nehemiah 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:2)


וַיִּבָּֽדְלוּ֙ זֶ֣רַע יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל מִכֹּ֖ל בְּנֵ֣י נֵכָ֑ר וַיַּעַמְד֗וּ וַיִּתְוַדּוּ֙ עַל־חַטֹּ֣אתֵיהֶ֔ם וַעֲוֺנ֖וֹת אֲבֹתֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the seed of Yisra᾽el separated themselves from all foreigners, and stood and confessed their sins, and the iniquities of their fathers.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the seed of Israel were separated from all of the foreigners [i.e., the idolators, and from their foreign wives (viz. Ezra 9 and 10)], and they arose and made confession for their sins and for the transgressions of their fathers.


###### Nehemiah 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:3)


וַיָּק֙וּמוּ֙ עַל־עׇמְדָ֔ם וַֽיִּקְרְא֗וּ בְּסֵ֨פֶר תּוֹרַ֧ת יְהֹוָ֛ה אֱלֹהֵיהֶ֖ם רְבִעִ֣ית הַיּ֑וֹם וּרְבִעִית֙ מִתְוַדִּ֣ים וּמִֽשְׁתַּחֲוִ֔ים לַיהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵיהֶֽם׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they stood up in their place, and read in the book of the Tora of the Lord their God one fourth part of the day; and another fourth part they confessed, and prostrated themselves before the Lord their God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they stood in their places and read in the scroll of the Torah of the L-rd their G d a quarter of the day, and (another) quarter, they made confession and prostrated themselves to the L-rd their G d.


###### Nehemiah 9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:4)


וַיָּ֜קׇם עַֽל־מַֽעֲלֵ֣ה הַלְוִיִּ֗ם יֵשׁ֨וּעַ וּבָנִ֜י קַדְמִיאֵ֧ל שְׁבַנְיָ֛ה בֻּנִּ֥י שֵׁרֵבְיָ֖ה בָּנִ֣י כְנָ֑נִי וַֽיִּזְעֲקוּ֙ בְּק֣וֹל גָּד֔וֹל אֶל־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then upon the stairs of the Levites stood Yeshua, and Bani, Qadmi᾽el, Shevanya, Bunni, Sherevya, Bani, and Kenani; and they cried with a loud voice to the Lord their God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there arose on the ascent of the Levites [(on which they stood when they chanted)] Yeshua, Bani, Kadmiel, Shevanyah, Buni, Sherevyah, Bani (and) Kenani, and they cried out in a great voice to the L-rd their G d.


###### Nehemiah 9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:5)


וַיֹּאמְר֣וּ הַלְוִיִּ֡ם יֵשׁ֣וּעַ וְ֠קַדְמִיאֵ֠ל בָּנִ֨י חֲשַׁבְנְיָ֜ה שֵׁרֵֽבְיָ֤ה הֽוֹדִיָּה֙ שְׁבַנְיָ֣ה פְתַֽחְיָ֔ה ק֗וּמוּ בָּרְכוּ֙ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶ֔ם מִן־הָעוֹלָ֖ם עַד־הָעוֹלָ֑ם וִיבָֽרְכוּ֙ שֵׁ֣ם כְּבֹדֶ֔ךָ וּמְרוֹמַ֥ם עַל־כׇּל־בְּרָכָ֖ה וּתְהִלָּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the Levites, Yeshua, and Qadmi᾽el, Bani, Ḥashavneya, Sherevya, Hodiyya, Shevanya, and Petaĥya said, Rise up and bless the Lord your God from everlasting to everlasting, and let them bless Thy glorious name, which is exalted above all blessing and praise.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Levites said — Yeshua, Kadmiel, Bani, Chahashav'nyah, Sherevyah, Hodiyah, Shevanyah (and) Petachyah — Arise and bless the L-rd your G d from the [beginning of] the world until the [end of] the world, and, [(addressing themselves to the Shechinah)], let them [(Israel)] bless the name of Your glory, which is exalted over all blessing and praise [(being their source)].


###### Nehemiah 9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:6)


אַתָּה־ה֣וּא יְהֹוָה֮ לְבַדֶּ֒ךָ֒ (את) [אַתָּ֣ה] עָשִׂ֡יתָ אֶֽת־הַשָּׁמַ֩יִם֩ שְׁמֵ֨י הַשָּׁמַ֜יִם וְכׇל־צְבָאָ֗ם הָאָ֜רֶץ וְכׇל־אֲשֶׁ֤ר עָלֶ֙יהָ֙ הַיַּמִּים֙ וְכׇל־אֲשֶׁ֣ר בָּהֶ֔ם וְאַתָּ֖ה מְחַיֶּ֣ה אֶת־כֻּלָּ֑ם וּצְבָ֥א הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם לְךָ֥ מִשְׁתַּחֲוִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou are Lord alone; Thou hast made heaven, the heaven of heavens, with all their host, the earth, and all things that are in it, the seas, and all that is therein, and Thou dost preserve them all; and the host of heaven worships Thee.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You are the L-rd alone. You made the heavens and the heavens of the heavens and all their hosts; the earth and all that is upon it; the seas and all that is in them. And You give life to all, and the host of heaven bows down to you.


###### Nehemiah 9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:7)


אַתָּה־הוּא֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֤ר בָּחַ֙רְתָּ֙ בְּאַבְרָ֔ם וְהוֹצֵאת֖וֹ מֵא֣וּר כַּשְׂדִּ֑ים וְשַׂ֥מְתָּ שְּׁמ֖וֹ אַבְרָהָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou art the Lord the God, who didst choose Avram, and didst bring him out of Ur of the Kasdim, and didst give him the name of Avraham;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You are the L-rd G d who chose Avram and took him out of Ur-Kasdim and made his name Avraham.


###### Nehemiah 9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:8)


וּמָצָ֣אתָ אֶת־לְבָבוֹ֮ נֶאֱמָ֣ן לְפָנֶ֒יךָ֒ וְכָר֨וֹת עִמּ֜וֹ הַבְּרִ֗ית לָתֵ֡ת אֶת־אֶ֩רֶץ֩ הַכְּנַעֲנִ֨י הַחִתִּ֜י הָאֱמֹרִ֧י וְהַפְּרִזִּ֛י וְהַיְבוּסִ֥י וְהַגִּרְגָּשִׁ֖י לָתֵ֣ת לְזַרְע֑וֹ וַתָּ֙קֶם֙ אֶת־דְּבָרֶ֔יךָ כִּ֥י צַדִּ֖יק אָֽתָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and didst find his heart faithful before Thee, and didst make a covenant with him to give the land of the Kena῾ani, the Ḥitti, the Emori, and the Perizzi, and the Yevusi, and the Girgashi, to give it to his seed, and Thou hast performed Thy words; for Thou art righteous:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And You found his heart faithful before You, and You made a covenant with him to give him the land of the Canaani, the Perizi, the Yevussi and the Girgashi to give to his seed. And You fulfilled Your word, for You are righteous.


###### Nehemiah 9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:9)


וַתֵּ֛רֶא אֶת־עֳנִ֥י אֲבֹתֵ֖ינוּ בְּמִצְרָ֑יִם וְאֶת־זַעֲקָתָ֥ם שָׁמַ֖עְתָּ עַל־יַם־סֽוּף׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and Thou didst see the affliction of our fathers in Miżrayim, and didst hear their cry by the sea of Suf;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And You saw the affliction of our fathers in Egypt, and You heard their outcry at the Red Sea.


###### Nehemiah 9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:10)


וַ֠תִּתֵּ֠ן אֹתֹ֨ת וּמֹֽפְתִ֜ים בְּפַרְעֹ֤ה וּבְכׇל־עֲבָדָיו֙ וּבְכׇל־עַ֣ם אַרְצ֔וֹ כִּ֣י יָדַ֔עְתָּ כִּ֥י הֵזִ֖ידוּ עֲלֵיהֶ֑ם וַתַּֽעַשׂ־לְךָ֥ שֵׁ֖ם כְּהַיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and didst show signs and wonders upon Par῾o, and on all his servants, and on all the people of his land: for Thou didst know that they dealt arrogantly against them. So didst Thou make for Thyself a name, as it is this day.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And You placed signs and wonders in Pharaoh and in all his servants and in all the people of his land, for You knew that they [(the Egyptians)] devised evil against them [(Israel) viz. Exodus 18:11], and You made Yourself a Name as of this day.


###### Nehemiah 9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:11)


וְהַיָּם֙ בָּקַ֣עְתָּ לִפְנֵיהֶ֔ם וַיַּֽעַבְר֥וּ בְתוֹךְ־הַיָּ֖ם בַּיַּבָּשָׁ֑ה וְֽאֶת־רֹ֨דְפֵיהֶ֜ם הִשְׁלַ֧כְתָּ בִמְצוֹלֹ֛ת כְּמוֹ־אֶ֖בֶן בְּמַ֥יִם עַזִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Thou didst divide the sea before them, so that they went through the midst of the sea on the dry land, and Thou didst throw their pursuers into the deeps, like a stone into the mighty waters.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And You split the sea before them, and they passed over in the midst of the sea on the dry land, and their pursuers You cast into the depths like a stone in mighty waters.


###### Nehemiah 9:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:12)


וּבְעַמּ֣וּד עָנָ֔ן הִנְחִיתָ֖ם יוֹמָ֑ם וּבְעַמּ֥וּד אֵשׁ֙ לַ֔יְלָה לְהָאִ֣יר לָהֶ֔ם אֶת־הַדֶּ֖רֶךְ אֲשֶׁ֥ר יֵֽלְכוּ־בָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Thou didst lead them with a pillar of cloud by day; and with a column of fire by night, to give them light on the way wherein they should go.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And with a pillar of cloud You led them by day and with a pillar of fire by night to light them on the way they were to go.


###### Nehemiah 9:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:13)


וְעַ֤ל הַר־סִינַי֙ יָרַ֔דְתָּ וְדַבֵּ֥ר עִמָּהֶ֖ם מִשָּׁמָ֑יִם וַתִּתֵּ֨ן לָהֶ֜ם מִשְׁפָּטִ֤ים יְשָׁרִים֙ וְתוֹר֣וֹת אֱמֶ֔ת חֻקִּ֥ים וּמִצְוֺ֖ת טוֹבִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Thou didst come down upon mount Sinay, and didst speak with them from heaven, and gavest them right judgments, and true Torot, good statutes and commandments:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And You descended on Mount Sinai to speak to them from heaven. And You gave them just judgments and laws of truth, good statutes and mitzvoth.


###### Nehemiah 9:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:14)


וְאֶת־שַׁבַּ֥ת קׇדְשְׁךָ֖ הוֹדַ֣עְתָּ לָהֶ֑ם וּמִצְו֤וֹת וְחֻקִּים֙ וְתוֹרָ֔ה צִוִּ֣יתָ לָהֶ֔ם בְּיַ֖ד מֹשֶׁ֥ה עַבְדֶּֽךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and Thou didst make known to them Thy holy sabbath, and didst command them precepts, statutes, and Tora, by the hand of Moshe Thy servant:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And You apprised them of Your holy Sabbath and You commanded unto them mitzvoth and statutes and Torah, by the hand of Moses, Your servant.


###### Nehemiah 9:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:15)


וְ֠לֶ֠חֶם מִשָּׁמַ֜יִם נָתַ֤תָּה לָהֶם֙ לִרְעָבָ֔ם וּמַ֗יִם מִסֶּ֛לַע הוֹצֵ֥אתָ לָהֶ֖ם לִצְמָאָ֑ם וַתֹּ֣אמֶר לָהֶ֗ם לָבוֹא֙ לָרֶ֣שֶׁת אֶת־הָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־נָשָׂ֥אתָ אֶת־יָדְךָ֖ לָתֵ֥ת לָהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and Thou didst give them bread from heaven for their hunger, and didst bring forth water for them out of the rock for their thirst, and didst promise them that they should go in to possess the land which Thou hadst sworn to give them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And You gave them bread from heaven in their hunger and water from a rock in their thirst. And You told them to come and inherit the land that You raised Your hand [i.e., that You swore] to give them.


###### Nehemiah 9:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:16)


וְהֵ֥ם וַאֲבֹתֵ֖ינוּ הֵזִ֑ידוּ וַיַּקְשׁוּ֙ אֶת־עׇרְפָּ֔ם וְלֹ֥א שָׁמְע֖וּ אֶל־מִצְוֺתֶֽיךָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But they and our fathers dealt arrogantly, and hardened their necks, and did not hearken to Thy commandments.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they and our fathers sinned wilfully and stiffened their necks and did not heed Your mitzvoth.


###### Nehemiah 9:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:17)


וַיְמָאֲנ֣וּ לִשְׁמֹ֗עַ וְלֹא־זָכְר֤וּ נִפְלְאֹתֶ֙יךָ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשִׂ֣יתָ עִמָּהֶ֔ם וַיַּקְשׁוּ֙ אֶת־עׇרְפָּ֔ם וַיִּתְּנוּ־רֹ֛אשׁ לָשׁ֥וּב לְעַבְדֻתָ֖ם בְּמִרְיָ֑ם וְאַתָּה֩ אֱל֨וֹהַּ סְלִיח֜וֹת חַנּ֧וּן וְרַח֛וּם אֶֽרֶךְ־אַפַּ֥יִם וְרַב־[חֶ֖סֶד] (וחסד) וְלֹ֥א עֲזַבְתָּֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they refused to obey; nor were they mindful of Thy wonders that Thou didst do among them; but they hardened their necks, and in their rebellion appointed a captain to return to their bondage: but Thou art a God ready to pardon, gracious and merciful, slow to anger, and great in love; so Thou didst not forsake them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they refused to listen and they did not remember the wonders that You had wrought for them. And they stiffened their necks, and they would make a leader for themselves to return to their servitude [of Egypt] in their rebelliousness. But You are a G-d of forgiveness, gracious and merciful, slow to anger and abundant in lovingkindness, and You did not forsake them.


###### Nehemiah 9:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:18)


אַ֗ף כִּֽי־עָשׂ֤וּ לָהֶם֙ עֵ֣גֶל מַסֵּכָ֔ה וַיֹּ֣אמְר֔וּ זֶ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ אֲשֶׁ֥ר הֶעֶלְךָ֖ מִמִּצְרָ֑יִם וַֽיַּעֲשׂ֔וּ נֶאָצ֖וֹת גְּדֹלֽוֹת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Even when they had made for themselves a molten calf, and had said, This is thy God who brought thee up out of Miżrayim, and had committed great blasphemies;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Even though they made for themselves a molten calf and said: "This is your god, who took you up from the land of Egypt"; and they committed great blasphemies.


###### Nehemiah 9:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:19)


וְאַתָּה֙ בְּרַחֲמֶ֣יךָ הָֽרַבִּ֔ים לֹ֥א עֲזַבְתָּ֖ם בַּמִּדְבָּ֑ר אֶת־עַמּ֣וּד הֶ֠עָנָ֠ן לֹא־סָ֨ר מֵעֲלֵיהֶ֤ם בְּיוֹמָם֙ לְהַנְחֹתָ֣ם בְּהַדֶּ֔רֶךְ וְאֶת־עַמּ֨וּד הָאֵ֤שׁ בְּלַ֙יְלָה֙ לְהָאִ֣יר לָהֶ֔ם וְאֶת־הַדֶּ֖רֶךְ אֲשֶׁ֥ר יֵֽלְכוּ־בָֽהּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
yet Thou in Thy manifold mercies didst not forsake them in the wilderness: the pillar of cloud departed not from them by day, to lead them in the way; nor did the column of fire by night, to show them light, and the way in which they should go.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Yet You, in Your many mercies, did not forsake them in the desert. The pillar of cloud did not depart from them by day to lead them on the way, and the pillar of fire by night to light them on the way they were to go.


###### Nehemiah 9:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:20)


וְרוּחֲךָ֙ הַטּוֹבָ֔ה נָתַ֖תָּ לְהַשְׂכִּילָ֑ם וּמַנְךָ֙ לֹא־מָנַ֣עְתָּ מִפִּיהֶ֔ם וּמַ֛יִם נָתַ֥תָּה לָהֶ֖ם לִצְמָאָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Thou didst give Thy good spirit to instruct them, and didst not withhold Thy manna from their mouth, and didst give them water for their thirst.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And You gave them Your goodly spirit to inform them, and You did not keep Your manna from their mouths, and You gave them water in their thirst.


###### Nehemiah 9:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:21)


וְאַרְבָּעִ֥ים שָׁנָ֛ה כִּלְכַּלְתָּ֥ם בַּמִּדְבָּ֖ר לֹ֣א חָסֵ֑רוּ שַׂלְמֹֽתֵיהֶם֙ לֹ֣א בָל֔וּ וְרַגְלֵיהֶ֖ם לֹ֥א בָצֵֽקוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For forty years Thou didst sustain them in the wilderness, so that they lacked nothing; their clothes did not grow old, and their feet did not swell.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And You fed them forty years in the desert. They lacked nothing. Their raiment did not wear out and their feet did not swell.


###### Nehemiah 9:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:22)


וַתִּתֵּ֨ן לָהֶ֤ם מַמְלָכוֹת֙ וַעֲמָמִ֔ים וַֽתַּחְלְקֵ֖ם לְפֵאָ֑ה וַיִּֽירְשׁ֞וּ אֶת־אֶ֣רֶץ סִיח֗וֹן וְאֶת־אֶ֙רֶץ֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ חֶשְׁבּ֔וֹן וְאֶת־אֶ֖רֶץ ע֥וֹג מֶֽלֶךְ־הַבָּשָֽׁן׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Thou didst give them kingdoms and nations, and didst allot it to them piecemeal: so that they possessed the land of Siĥon, and the land of the king of Ḥeshbon, and the land of ῾Og king of Bashan.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And You gave them kingdoms and peoples, and You set them apart [(in isolation from the other peoples)]. And they inherited the land of Sichon and the land of the king of Cheshbon and the land of Og, the king of the Bashan.


###### Nehemiah 9:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:23)


וּבְנֵיהֶ֣ם הִרְבִּ֔יתָ כְּכֹכְבֵ֖י הַשָּׁמָ֑יִם וַתְּבִיאֵם֙ אֶל־הָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־אָמַ֥רְתָּ לַאֲבֹתֵיהֶ֖ם לָב֥וֹא לָרָֽשֶׁת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Their children also Thou didst multiply like the stars of heaven, and didst bring them into the land, concerning which Thou hadst promised to their fathers, that they should go in to possess it.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And You multiplied their children like the stars of the heavens, and You brought them to the land that you told their fathers to come to and inherit.


###### Nehemiah 9:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:24)


וַיָּבֹ֤אוּ הַבָּנִים֙ וַיִּֽירְשׁ֣וּ אֶת־הָאָ֔רֶץ וַתַּכְנַ֨ע לִפְנֵיהֶ֜ם אֶת־יֹשְׁבֵ֤י הָאָ֙רֶץ֙ הַכְּנַ֣עֲנִ֔ים וַֽתִּתְּנֵ֖ם בְּיָדָ֑ם וְאֶת־מַלְכֵיהֶם֙ וְאֶת־עַֽמְמֵ֣י הָאָ֔רֶץ לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת בָּהֶ֖ם כִּרְצוֹנָֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So the children went in and possessed the land, and Thou didst subdue before them the inhabitants of the land, the Kena῾anim, and didst give them into their hands, with their kings, and the peoples of the land, so that they might do with them as they pleased.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the children came and inherited the land, and You humbled before them the dwellers of the land, the Canaanites, and You delivered them into their hands, and their kings and the peoples of the land, to do with them as they willed.


###### Nehemiah 9:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:25)


וַֽיִּלְכְּד֞וּ עָרִ֣ים בְּצוּרֹת֮ וַאֲדָמָ֣ה שְׁמֵנָה֒ וַיִּֽירְשׁ֡וּ בָּתִּ֣ים מְלֵֽאִים־כׇּל־ט֠וּב בֹּר֨וֹת חֲצוּבִ֜ים כְּרָמִ֧ים וְזֵיתִ֛ים וְעֵ֥ץ מַאֲכָ֖ל לָרֹ֑ב וַיֹּאכְל֤וּ וַֽיִּשְׂבְּעוּ֙ וַיַּשְׁמִ֔ינוּ וַיִּֽתְעַדְּנ֖וּ בְּטוּבְךָ֥ הַגָּדֽוֹל׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they took fortified cities, and a fat land, and possessed houses full of all goods, wells already hewn, vineyards, and oliveyards, and fruit trees in abundance: so they did eat, and were sated, and became fat, and delighted themselves in Thy great bounty.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they conquered fortified cities and fat soil and they inherited houses full of all good (and) hewn-out wells, vineyards, olive yards, and fruit trees in abundance. And they ate and were sated and they fattened and luxuriated in Your great good.


###### Nehemiah 9:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:26)


וַיַּמְר֨וּ וַֽיִּמְרְד֜וּ בָּ֗ךְ וַיַּשְׁלִ֤כוּ אֶת־תּוֹרָֽתְךָ֙ אַחֲרֵ֣י גַוָּ֔ם וְאֶת־נְבִיאֶ֣יךָ הָרָ֔גוּ אֲשֶׁר־הֵעִ֥ידוּ בָ֖ם לַהֲשִׁיבָ֣ם אֵלֶ֑יךָ וַֽיַּעֲשׂ֔וּ נֶאָצ֖וֹת גְּדוֹלֹֽת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Nevertheless they were disobedient, and rebelled against Thee, and cast Thy Tora behind their backs, and slew Thy prophets who forewarned them to turn them to Thee, and they committed great blasphemies.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they rose up and rebelled against You, and they cast Your Torah behind their backs, and they killed Your prophets who exhorted them to return them to You, and they committed great blasphemies.


###### Nehemiah 9:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:27)


וַֽתִּתְּנֵם֙ בְּיַ֣ד צָֽרֵיהֶ֔ם וַיָּצֵ֖רוּ לָהֶ֑ם וּבְעֵ֤ת צָֽרָתָם֙ יִצְעֲק֣וּ אֵלֶ֔יךָ וְאַתָּה֙ מִשָּׁמַ֣יִם תִּשְׁמָ֔ע וּֽכְרַחֲמֶ֣יךָ הָֽרַבִּ֗ים תִּתֵּ֤ן לָהֶם֙ מֽוֹשִׁיעִ֔ים וְיוֹשִׁיע֖וּם מִיַּ֥ד צָרֵיהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore Thou didst deliver them into the hand of their enemies, who vexed them: and in the time of their trouble, when they cried to Thee, Thou didst hear them from heaven; and according to Thy manifold mercies Thou didst give them rescuers, who rescued them out of the hand of their enemies.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And You delivered them into the hand of their oppressors and they afflicted them. And in the time of their affliction they would cry out to You. And You, in heaven, would hear. And in Your many mercies, You would give them saviors and they would save them from the hand of their oppressors.


###### Nehemiah 9:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:28)


וּכְנ֣וֹחַ לָהֶ֔ם יָשׁ֕וּבוּ לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת רַ֖ע לְפָנֶ֑יךָ וַתַּֽעַזְבֵ֞ם בְּיַ֤ד אֹֽיְבֵיהֶם֙ וַיִּרְדּ֣וּ בָהֶ֔ם וַיָּשׁ֙וּבוּ֙ וַיִּזְעָק֔וּךָ וְאַתָּ֞ה מִשָּׁמַ֧יִם תִּשְׁמַ֛ע וְתַצִּילֵ֥ם כְּֽרַחֲמֶ֖יךָ רַבּ֥וֹת עִתִּֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But after they had rest, they did evil again before Thee: therefore Thou didst leave them in the hand of their enemies, so that they had dominion over them: yet when they returned, and cried to Thee, Thou didst hear them from heaven; and many times didst Thou deliver them according to thy mercies;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when (their lot) was eased, they would revert to doing evil before You and You would leave them in the hands of their foes. And they would oppress them and they would again cry out to You. And You, from heaven, would hear. And You would save them in Your mercies many times.


###### Nehemiah 9:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:29)


וַתָּ֨עַד בָּהֶ֜ם לַהֲשִׁיבָ֣ם אֶל־תּוֹרָתֶ֗ךָ וְהֵ֨מָּה הֵזִ֜ידוּ וְלֹא־שָׁמְע֤וּ לְמִצְוֺתֶ֙יךָ֙ וּבְמִשְׁפָּטֶ֣יךָ חָֽטְאוּ־בָ֔ם אֲשֶׁר־יַעֲשֶׂ֥ה אָדָ֖ם וְחָיָ֣ה בָהֶ֑ם וַיִּתְּנ֤וּ כָתֵף֙ סוֹרֶ֔רֶת וְעׇרְפָּ֥ם הִקְשׁ֖וּ וְלֹ֥א שָׁמֵֽעוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and didst forewarn them, that Thou mightest bring them back to Thy Tora: yet they acted presumptuously, and hearkened not to Thy commandments, but sinned against Thy judgments, (which if a man do, he shall live by them;) and they turned a stubborn shoulder, and hardened their neck, and would not hear.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And You would exhort them to return them to Your Torah. But they acted wilfully and would not heed Your mitzvoth and sinned in Your judgments, which, if a man does them, he lives through them. And they lifted a recalcitrant shoulder and stiffened their necks and would not pay heed.


###### Nehemiah 9:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:30)


וַתִּמְשֹׁ֤ךְ עֲלֵיהֶם֙ שָׁנִ֣ים רַבּ֔וֹת וַתָּ֨עַד בָּ֧ם בְּרוּחֲךָ֛ בְּיַד־נְבִיאֶ֖יךָ וְלֹ֣א הֶאֱזִ֑ינוּ וַֽתִּתְּנֵ֔ם בְּיַ֖ד עַמֵּ֥י הָאֲרָצֹֽת׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yet many years didst Thou bear with them, and didst forewarn them by Thy spirit in Thy prophets: yet they would not give ear: therefore Thou didst give them into the hand of the peoples of the lands.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And You drew out [Your mercies] to them for many years, and You warned them with Your spirit through Your prophets, but they would not listen.


###### Nehemiah 9:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:31)


וּֽבְרַחֲמֶ֧יךָ הָרַבִּ֛ים לֹֽא־עֲשִׂיתָ֥ם כָּלָ֖ה וְלֹ֣א עֲזַבְתָּ֑ם כִּ֛י אֵֽל־חַנּ֥וּן וְרַח֖וּם אָֽתָּה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yet for Thy great mercies’ sake Thou didst not utterly consume them, or forsake them; for Thou art a gracious and merciful God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And in Your many mercies You did not make an end of them and You did not forsake them, for You are a gracious and merciful G-d.


###### Nehemiah 9:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:32)


וְעַתָּ֣ה אֱ֠לֹהֵ֠ינוּ הָאֵ֨ל הַגָּד֜וֹל הַגִּבּ֣וֹר וְהַנּוֹרָא֮ שׁוֹמֵ֣ר הַבְּרִ֣ית וְהַחֶ֒סֶד֒ אַל־יִמְעַ֣ט לְפָנֶ֡יךָ אֵ֣ת כׇּל־הַתְּלָאָ֣ה אֲֽשֶׁר־מְ֠צָאַ֠תְנוּ לִמְלָכֵ֨ינוּ לְשָׂרֵ֧ינוּ וּלְכֹהֲנֵ֛ינוּ וְלִנְבִיאֵ֥ינוּ וְלַאֲבֹתֵ֖ינוּ וּלְכׇל־עַמֶּ֑ךָ מִימֵי֙ מַלְכֵ֣י אַשּׁ֔וּר עַ֖ד הַיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now therefore, our God, the great, the mighty, and the terrible God, who keepest covenant and troth, let not all the trouble seem little before Thee, that has come upon us, on our kings, on our princes, and on our priests, and on our prophets, and on our fathers, and on all Thy people, since the time of the kings of Ashshur to this day.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, our G-d, the great, and mighty, and awesome G-d, Keeper of the covenant and the lovingkindness, let (it) not be little before You, all the suffering that has befallen our kings, our leaders, our priests, our prophets, our fathers, and all Your people, from the days of the kings of Ashur until this day.


###### Nehemiah 9:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:33)


וְאַתָּ֣ה צַדִּ֔יק עַ֖ל כׇּל־הַבָּ֣א עָלֵ֑ינוּ כִּֽי־אֱמֶ֥ת עָשִׂ֖יתָ וַאֲנַ֥חְנוּ הִרְשָֽׁעְנוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yet Thou art just in all that is brought upon us; for Thou hast done right, but we have done wickedly:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And You are righteous in all [the affliction] that has come upon us. for You have wrought truth and we have been wicked.


###### Nehemiah 9:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:34)


וְאֶת־מְלָכֵ֤ינוּ שָׂרֵ֙ינוּ֙ כֹּהֲנֵ֣ינוּ וַאֲבֹתֵ֔ינוּ לֹ֥א עָשׂ֖וּ תּוֹרָתֶ֑ךָ וְלֹ֤א הִקְשִׁ֙יבוּ֙ אֶל־מִצְוֺתֶ֔יךָ וּלְעֵ֣דְוֺתֶ֔יךָ אֲשֶׁ֥ר הַעִידֹ֖תָ בָּהֶֽם׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
nor have our kings, our princes, our priests, and our fathers, kept Thy Tora, or hearkened to Thy commandments and Thy testimonies, with which Thou didst testify against them.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And our kings, our leaders, our priests, and our fathers have not observed Your Torah and have not heeded Your mitzvoth and Your exhortations wherewith You exhorted them.


###### Nehemiah 9:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:35)


וְהֵ֣ם בְּמַלְכוּתָם֩ וּבְטוּבְךָ֨ הָרָ֜ב אֲשֶׁר־נָתַ֣תָּ לָהֶ֗ם וּבְאֶ֨רֶץ הָרְחָבָ֧ה וְהַשְּׁמֵנָ֛ה אֲשֶׁר־נָתַ֥תָּ לִפְנֵיהֶ֖ם לֹ֣א עֲבָד֑וּךָ וְֽלֹא־שָׁ֔בוּ מִמַּֽעַלְלֵיהֶ֖ם הָרָעִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For they have not served Thee in their kingdom, and in Thy great bounty that Thou didst give them, and in the large and fat land which Thou didst give before them, nor did they turn from their wicked works.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they, in their kingship and in their abundant good which You accorded them and in the broad, fat land that You placed before them did not serve You and did not repent of their wicked deeds.


###### Nehemiah 9:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:36)


הִנֵּ֛ה אֲנַ֥חְנוּ הַיּ֖וֹם עֲבָדִ֑ים וְהָאָ֜רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־נָתַ֣תָּה לַאֲבֹתֵ֗ינוּ לֶאֱכֹ֤ל אֶת־פִּרְיָהּ֙ וְאֶת־טוּבָ֔הּ הִנֵּ֛ה אֲנַ֥חְנוּ עֲבָדִ֖ים עָלֶֽיהָ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, we are servants this day, and as for the land that Thou didst give to our fathers to eat its fruit and its bounty, behold, we are servants in it;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Behold, we are servants this day; and the land that You gave our fathers, to eat its fruit and its good — behold, we are servants upon it.


###### Nehemiah 9:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/9:37)


וּתְבוּאָתָ֣הּ מַרְבָּ֗ה לַמְּלָכִ֛ים אֲשֶׁר־נָתַ֥תָּה עָלֵ֖ינוּ בְּחַטֹּאותֵ֑ינוּ וְעַ֣ל גְּ֠וִיֹּתֵ֠ינוּ מֹשְׁלִ֤ים וּבִבְהֶמְתֵּ֙נוּ֙ כִּרְצוֹנָ֔ם וּבְצָרָ֥ה גְדֹלָ֖ה אֲנָֽחְנוּ׃{פ}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and its rich yield goes to the kings whom Thou hast set over us because of our sins: also they have power over our bodies, and over our cattle, at their pleasure, and we are in great distress.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And its produce adds [luxuries (through the taxes imposed upon us)] to the [gentile] kings that You have placed over us in our sins. And they rule over our bodies and our beasts at their will, and we find ourselves in great distress.

###### Nehemiah 10:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:29)


וּשְׁאָ֣ר הָעָ֡ם הַכֹּהֲנִ֣ים הַ֠לְוִיִּ֠ם הַשּׁוֹעֲרִ֨ים הַמְשֹׁרְרִ֜ים הַנְּתִינִ֗ים וְֽכׇל־הַנִּבְדָּ֞ל מֵעַמֵּ֤י הָאֲרָצוֹת֙ אֶל־תּוֹרַ֣ת הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים נְשֵׁיהֶ֖ם בְּנֵיהֶ֣ם וּבְנֹתֵיהֶ֑ם כֹּ֖ל יוֹדֵ֥עַ מֵבִֽין׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the rest of the people, the priests, the Levites, the gate keepers, the singers, the temple servants, and all they who have separated themselves from the peoples of the lands to the Tora of God, their wives, their sons, and their daughters, everyone having knowledge, and having understanding;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the rest of the people, the priests, the Levites, the gatekeepers, the singers, the Nethinim, and all [the proselytes] who had separated themselves from the peoples of the lands unto the Torah of G-d, their wives, their sons, their daughters — all who were possessed of understanding —


###### Nehemiah 10:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:30)


מַחֲזִיקִ֣ים עַל־אֲחֵיהֶם֮ אַדִּירֵיהֶם֒ וּבָאִ֞ים בְּאָלָ֣ה וּבִשְׁבוּעָ֗ה לָלֶ֙כֶת֙ בְּתוֹרַ֣ת הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר נִתְּנָ֔ה בְּיַ֖ד מֹשֶׁ֣ה עֶֽבֶד־הָֽאֱלֹהִ֑ים וְלִשְׁמ֣וֹר וְלַעֲשׂ֗וֹת אֶת־כׇּל־מִצְוֺת֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֲדֹנֵ֔ינוּ וּמִשְׁפָּטָ֖יו וְחֻקָּֽיו׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
join with their brethren, their nobles, and enter into a curse, and into an oath, to walk in the Tora of God, which was given by Moshe the servant of God, and to observe and do all the commandments of the Lord our Lord, and his judgments and his statutes;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
confirmed [the covenant of] their brethren, their dignitaries, and they undertook with oath and vow to walk in the Torah of G d that had been given by the hand of Moses, the servant of G d, and to keep and do all the mitzvoth of the L-rd, our Master, and His mitzvoth and His statutes,


###### Nehemiah 10:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:31)


וַאֲשֶׁ֛ר לֹא־נִתֵּ֥ן בְּנֹתֵ֖ינוּ לְעַמֵּ֣י הָאָ֑רֶץ וְאֶת־בְּנֹ֣תֵיהֶ֔ם לֹ֥א נִקַּ֖ח לְבָנֵֽינוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and that we will not give our daughters to the peoples of the land, or take their daughters for our sons:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
and not to give our daughters to the people of the land, and not to take their daughters for our sons,


###### Nehemiah 10:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:32)


וְעַמֵּ֣י הָאָ֡רֶץ הַֽמְבִיאִים֩ אֶת־הַמַּקָּח֨וֹת וְכׇל־שֶׁ֜בֶר בְּי֤וֹם הַשַּׁבָּת֙ לִמְכּ֔וֹר לֹא־נִקַּ֥ח מֵהֶ֛ם בַּשַּׁבָּ֖ת וּבְי֣וֹם קֹ֑דֶשׁ וְנִטֹּ֛שׁ אֶת־הַשָּׁנָ֥ה הַשְּׁבִיעִ֖ית וּמַשָּׁ֥א כׇל־יָֽד׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and if the peoples of the land bring in wares or any grain on the sabbath day to sell, that we will not buy it of them on the sabbath, or on the holy day: and that we will forego the produce of the seventh year, and the exaction of every debt.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
and not to purchase on Sabbath or holy day from the peoples of the land who bring merchandise and all (kinds of) produce to sell on the Sabbath, and to let the land lie fallow on the seventh year [viz. Exodus 23:11] and [to relinquish] every claim [viz. Deuteronomy 15:2].


###### Nehemiah 10:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:33)


וְהֶעֱמַ֤דְנוּ עָלֵ֙ינוּ֙ מִצְוֺ֔ת לָתֵ֥ת עָלֵ֛ינוּ שְׁלִישִׁ֥ית הַשֶּׁ֖קֶל בַּשָּׁנָ֑ה לַעֲבֹדַ֖ת בֵּ֥ית אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
We also lay ordinances on ourselves, to charge ourselves yearly with the third part of a shekel for the service of the house of our God;


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And we took upon ourselves as mitzvoth to give for ourselves a third of a shekel a year [(beyond the prescribed half-shekel [viz. Exodus 30:13])] for the service of the house of our G-d:


###### Nehemiah 10:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:34)


לְלֶ֣חֶם הַֽמַּעֲרֶ֡כֶת וּמִנְחַ֣ת הַתָּמִ֣יד וּלְעוֹלַ֣ת הַ֠תָּמִ֠יד הַשַּׁבָּת֨וֹת הֶחֳדָשִׁ֜ים לַמּוֹעֲדִ֗ים וְלַקֳּדָשִׁים֙ וְלַ֣חַטָּא֔וֹת לְכַפֵּ֖ר עַל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וְכֹ֖ל מְלֶ֥אכֶת בֵּית־אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ׃{ס}


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for the showbread, and for the continual meal offering, and for the continual burnt offering, of the sabbaths, of the new moons, for the appointed seasons, and for the holy things, and for the sin offerings to make atonement for Yisra᾽el, and for all the work of the house of our God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
for the show bread and the perpetual oblation and the perpetual burnt-offering and the [offerings for] the Sabbaths, the New Moons, the festivals, and the [communal] (peace-) offerings and sin-offerings to atone for Israel, and for all the [silver] work of the house of our G-d.


###### Nehemiah 10:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:35)


וְהַגּוֹרָל֨וֹת הִפַּ֜לְנוּ עַל־קֻרְבַּ֣ן הָעֵצִ֗ים הַכֹּהֲנִ֣ים הַלְוִיִּם֮ וְהָעָם֒ לְ֠הָבִ֠יא לְבֵ֨ית אֱלֹהֵ֧ינוּ לְבֵית־אֲבֹתֵ֛ינוּ לְעִתִּ֥ים מְזֻמָּנִ֖ים שָׁנָ֣ה בְשָׁנָ֑ה לְבַעֵ֗ר עַל־מִזְבַּח֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֔ינוּ כַּכָּת֖וּב בַּתּוֹרָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And we have cast lots among the priests, the Levites, and the people, for the wood offering, to bring it to the house of our God, according to our fathers’ houses, at times appointed, year by year, to burn upon the altar of the Lord our God, as it is written in the Tora:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And we cast lots for the offering of wood [for the altar] — the priests, the Levites, and the people — to bring to the house of our G d, the house of [holiness] of our fathers, (to bring it) at set times, year by year, to burn on the altar of the L-rd our G d, as written in the Torah.


###### Nehemiah 10:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:36)


וּלְהָבִ֞יא אֶת־בִּכּוּרֵ֣י אַדְמָתֵ֗נוּ וּבִכּוּרֵ֛י כׇּל־פְּרִ֥י כׇל־עֵ֖ץ שָׁנָ֣ה בְשָׁנָ֑ה לְבֵ֖ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and to bring the firstfruits of our ground, and the firstfruits of all fruit of all trees, year by year, to the house of the Lord:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [we undertook] to bring the first-fruits of our land and the first-fruits of all fruit-trees year by year to the house of our L-rd [(the latter, a rabbinical enactment)]


###### Nehemiah 10:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:37)


וְאֶת־בְּכֹר֤וֹת בָּנֵ֙ינוּ֙ וּבְהֶמְתֵּ֔נוּ כַּכָּת֖וּב בַּתּוֹרָ֑ה וְאֶת־בְּכוֹרֵ֨י בְקָרֵ֜ינוּ וְצֹאנֵ֗ינוּ לְהָבִיא֙ לְבֵ֣ית אֱלֹהֵ֔ינוּ לַכֹּ֣הֲנִ֔ים הַמְשָׁרְתִ֖ים בְּבֵ֥ית אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
also the firstborn of our sons, and of our cattle, as it is written in the Tora, and the firstlings of our herds and of our flocks, to bring to the house of our God, to the priests who minister in the house of our God:


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
and (to bring) the [redemption monies] of our first-born sons and of our beasts [i.e., first-born of asses], as written in the Torah, and the first-born of our cattle and of our flocks, to bring (them) to the house of our G-d, to the priests who serve in the house of our G-d,


###### Nehemiah 10:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:38)


וְאֶת־רֵאשִׁ֣ית עֲרִיסֹתֵ֣ינוּ וּ֠תְרוּמֹתֵ֠ינוּ וּפְרִ֨י כׇל־עֵ֜ץ תִּיר֣וֹשׁ וְיִצְהָ֗ר נָבִ֤יא לַכֹּהֲנִים֙ אֶל־לִשְׁכ֣וֹת בֵּית־אֱלֹהֵ֔ינוּ וּמַעְשַׂ֥ר אַדְמָתֵ֖נוּ לַלְוִיִּ֑ם וְהֵם֙ הַלְוִיִּ֔ם הַֽמְעַשְּׂרִ֔ים בְּכֹ֖ל עָרֵ֥י עֲבֹדָתֵֽנוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and that we shall bring the firstfruits of our dough, and our offerings, and the fruit of all manner of trees, of wine and oil, to the priests, to the chambers of the house of our God; and the tithes of our ground to the Levites; for they, the Levites receive the tithes in all the cities of our tillage.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
and to bring the first of our dough and our tithes and the fruit of every tree, wine and oil, to the priests, to the chambers of the house of our G-d, and (to bring) the tithe of our land to the Levites, the Levites who take their tithes in all the cities of our work [(in Eretz Israel)].


###### Nehemiah 10:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:39)


וְהָיָ֨ה הַכֹּהֵ֧ן בֶּֽן־אַהֲרֹ֛ן עִם־הַלְוִיִּ֖ם בַּעְשֵׂ֣ר הַלְוִיִּ֑ם וְהַלְוִיִּ֞ם יַעֲל֨וּ אֶת־מַעֲשַׂ֤ר הַֽמַּעֲשֵׂר֙ לְבֵ֣ית אֱלֹהֵ֔ינוּ אֶל־הַלְּשָׁכ֖וֹת לְבֵ֥ית הָאוֹצָֽר׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest the son of Aharon shall be with the Levites, when the Levites take tithes: and the Levites shall bring up the tithe of the tithes to the house of our God, to the chambers, into the treasure house.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [we undertook that] a priest, a son [i.e., descendant] of Aaron be with the Levites, when the Levites tithed. And the Levites would bring a tithe of the tithe to the house of our G-d to the chambers [(of the priests, [who would not leave the Temple])], to the store-house.


###### Nehemiah 10:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/10:40)


כִּ֣י אֶל־הַ֠לְּשָׁכ֠וֹת יָבִ֨יאוּ בְנֵי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֜ל וּבְנֵ֣י הַלֵּוִ֗י אֶת־תְּרוּמַ֣ת הַדָּגָן֮ הַתִּיר֣וֹשׁ וְהַיִּצְהָר֒ וְשָׁם֙ כְּלֵ֣י הַמִּקְדָּ֔שׁ וְהַכֹּהֲנִים֙ הַמְשָׁ֣רְתִ֔ים וְהַשּׁוֹעֲרִ֖ים וְהַמְשֹׁרְרִ֑ים וְלֹ֥א נַעֲזֹ֖ב אֶת־בֵּ֥ית אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the children of Yisra᾽el and the children of Levi shall bring the offering of the corn, of the new wine, and the oil, to the chambers, where are the vessels of the sanctuary, and the priests who minister, and the gate keepers, and the singers: and we will not forsake the house of our God.


**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For to the chambers the sons of Israel and the sons of the Levites would bring the tithe of the grain, the wine, and the oil. And there were found the Temple vessels, and the ministering priests, and the gatekeepers and the singers — so that we not forsake the house of our G-d.



## Chapter 11

###### Nehemiah 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Nehemiah/r/11:1)


וַיֵּשְׁב֥וּ שָׂרֵֽי־הָעָ֖ם בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וּשְׁאָ֣ר הָ֠עָ֠ם הִפִּ֨ילוּ גוֹרָל֜וֹת לְהָבִ֣יא**׀** אֶחָ֣ד מִן־הָעֲשָׂרָ֗ה לָשֶׁ֙בֶת֙ בִּֽירוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ עִ֣יר הַקֹּ֔דֶשׁ וְתֵ֥שַׁע הַיָּד֖וֹת בֶּעָרִֽים׃


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi Ketuvim by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
